We introduce DASE (Deliberative Adaptive Stopping Ensemble), a stopping heuristic for iterative ensemble deliberation that commits early on genuine consensus and applies a global-frequency fallback on fragmented evidence. Two configurations are evaluated: a persistence heuristic and DASE-Spatial (arena half-width W). Three contributions. (1) DASE produces a commit-type routing partition complementary to verbalized single-call confidence. On a contamination-controlled corpus (AIME 2010-2023, N=254, 3 seeds), a 120B ensemble achieves a 24.8 pp routing gap (right-wall 97.1% vs. left-wall 73.6%), statistically equivalent to Opus 4.6 Standard verbalized confidence at coverage-matched threshold (25.7 pp gap; bootstrap CI on difference: [-12.0, +10.3] pp, p=0.873). The two mechanisms disagree on 27% of routing assignments, establishing them as complements rather than substitutes; every DASE decision is accompanied by a machine-readable deliberation record. (2) Adaptive stopping, not injection bandwidth, drives accuracy gains. On AIME-300, bandwidth accounts for only 0.3 pp (ns); on GPQA-Extended, 4.4 pp bandwidth versus 5.0 pp stopping effect. DASE-Spatial ties Debate-Dense at its optimal budget using one-tenth the injection bandwidth and identifies that budget automatically; W=8 (65.0%) significantly outperforms W=4 (59.3%) on AIME-300 (adj p=0.0042). (3) Injection-based methods exhibit a retrospective accuracy-vs-inference inverted-U on both benchmarks; this pattern is hypothesis-generating for future work.

</details>


### [110] [Hierarchical Support Vector State Partitioning for Distilling Black Box Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04254)
*Senne Deproost,Mehrdad Asadi,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SVSP方法，通过线性SVM分割状态空间构建可解释子策略，在减少82.1%子策略数量的同时，比原始TD3策略提升2.8%平均回报，比VSP方法提升7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略蒸馏方法（如VSP）依赖批评者驱动的状态划分，导致子策略数量过多且可解释性不足，需要更灵活、紧凑的策略蒸馏方法。

Method: 使用线性支持向量机（SVM）对蒸馏数据集中的状态-动作对进行分割，将原始黑盒策略划分为一组人类可解释的子策略，构建紧凑的结构化表示。

Result: 平均回报比VSP提升7.4%，比原始TD3策略提升2.8%；子策略数量相比VSP减少82.1%。

Conclusion: SVSP为更灵活的蒸馏形式铺平了道路，决策边界和替代模型均可在一定范围内选择，兼顾了性能与可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce State Vector Space Partitioning (SVSP), a novel method to mimic a black box reinforcement learning policy using a set of human-interpretable subpolicies. By partitioning a distillation dataset of state action pairs with linear support vector machine splits, SVSP constructs a compact and structured representation of the original policy. Our method improves mean return by +7.4\% over previous critic driven state partitioning attempts such as Voronoi State Partitioning (VSP) and +2.8\% over the original TD3 policy, while reducing the number of required subpolicies against VSP by 82.1\%. Our results pave the path towards a more flexible form of distillation where both the decision boundary and surrogate models can be chosen within a margin of the original black box behavior.

</details>


### [111] [HUGO-CS: A Hybrid-Labeled, Uncertainty-Aware, General-Purpose, Observational Dataset for Cold Spray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04257)
*Stephen Price,Kyle Miller,Marco Musto,Kenneth Kroenlein,James Saal,Kyle Tsaknopoulos,Elke A. Rundensteiner,Danielle L. Cote*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HUGO框架，从文献中提取冷喷涂实验数据，构建了包含4,383个实验、144个特征的数据集HUGO-CS，比此前最大数据集大30倍。HUGO结合LLM自动标注与人工审核，引入分层风险缓解机制平衡效率与准确性，最终释放了高质量标注子集与全部代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷喷涂工艺优化因参数相互依赖、缺乏大规模机器可读数据而困难。文献中实验结果报告不一致（表格/图片）、单位不统一，难以规模化利用。现有数据集规模极小（仅137个样本），亟需大规模结构化数据集支持建模。

Method: 提出HUGO框架：1) 混合标注：结合LLM自动标注与人工手动精炼；2) 分层风险缓解(HRM)：对高错误风险的LLM输出路由至人工审核，低风险记录保留为自动标注；3) 后处理：合并分类描述符、将报告 feedstock 化学成分映射为结构化连续组成、跨来源单位归一化。完全手动提取每篇文档平均需91分钟，HUGO大幅提升了效率。

Result: 构建了HUGO-CS数据集：4,383个冷喷涂实验、144个特征、来自1,124篇文献，比此前最大数据集大30倍。其中1,765个实验为手工标注，构成高质量基准子集。所有代码与数据集以CC-BY许可发布。

Conclusion: HUGO框架有效解决了从科学文献中大规模提取实验数据的关键挑战（标注效率、准确性、单位/格式不统一），其产出数据集HUGO-CS将极大促进冷喷涂工艺的数据驱动建模与优化。

Abstract: Cold spraying is an increasingly common approach for repairing and manufacturing components due to its solid-state manufacturing capabilities. However, process optimization remains difficult due to many interdependent parameters and the lack of large-scale, machine-readable data to support modeling. While the scientific literature contains many relevant experiments, results are inconsistently reported (often in tables and figures) and use non-uniform units, limiting utilization at scale. To address these limitations, this work presents HUGO-CS, a literature-derived dataset of 4,383 cold-spray experiments with 144 features from 1,124 sources, exceeding the previous largest dataset (137 samples) by 30x. With completely manual extraction requiring an average of 91 minutes per document, this work designs and leverages a Hybrid-labeled, Uncertainty-aware, General-purpose, Observational extraction framework, called HUGO, to support this extraction. HUGO combines automated LLM-based labeling with targeted manual label refinement to handle this experimental result extraction process from scientific literature. To balance labeling efficiency with extraction accuracy, HUGO introduces a Hierarchical Risk Mitigation (HRM) to route LLM outputs with a high risk of potential errors for manual review, while retaining low-risk records as auto-labeled. Lastly, HUGO post-processing consolidates categorical descriptors, maps reported feedstock chemistries into structured continuous compositions, and normalizes units across sources. Of the 4,383 reported experiments, 1,765 are hand-labeled, providing a high-quality labeled subset for benchmarking, error analysis, and higher-fidelity data points. All code to replicate this work, along with the complete HUGO-CS dataset, are released under a CC-BY license at https://github.com/sprice134/HUGO.

</details>


### [112] [Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266)
*Etienne Gauthier,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准迭代RLHF中因忽略策略对奖励模型参数影响的反馈循环而导致的"对齐崩溃"问题，并提出了一种可扩展的一阶近似方法FPO来恢复该梯度项，从而在Llama-3.2-1B上防止对齐崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准迭代RLHF假设奖励模型是静态的，但在迭代部署中策略生成的数据会被用于重新训练RM，形成反馈循环。这种忽略导致策略系统性地利用RM的盲点，产生低质量高奖励的输出，进而使反馈强化了RM自身的错误，引发对齐崩溃。

Method: 基于Stackelberg博弈形式化策略与RM的交互，推导出策略真实优化梯度的解析分解：标准策略梯度 + 参数引导项（policy's parameter-steering term）。提出FPO，通过正则化策略对RM参数更新的引导效应来恢复缺失项，并采用可扩展的一阶近似实现。

Result: 在受控环境和基于Llama-3.2-1B的LLM对齐流水线上，FPO有效防止了对齐崩溃，相比标准迭代RLHF产生更高质量、更可靠的输出。

Conclusion: 标准迭代RLHF因忽视策略-RM反馈循环中的策略引导效应而导致对齐崩溃；FPO通过恢复该梯度项提供了一种有效的机制设计干预方案，具有实际可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) typically assumes a static or non-strategic reward model (RM). In iterative deployment, however, the policy generates the data on which the RM is retrained, creating a feedback loop. Building on the Stackelberg game formulation of this interaction, we derive an analytical decomposition of the policy's true optimization gradient into a standard policy gradient and a parameter-steering term that captures the policy's influence on the RM's future parameters. We show that standard iterative RLHF, which drops this steering term entirely, suffers from alignment collapse: the policy systematically exploits the RM's blind spots, producing low-quality, high-reward outputs whose feedback reinforces the very errors it exploits. To mitigate this, we propose foresighted policy optimization (FPO), a mechanism-design intervention that restores the missing steering term by regularizing the policy's parameter-steering effect on RM updates. We instantiate FPO via a scalable first-order approximation and demonstrate that it prevents alignment collapse on both controlled environments and an LLM alignment pipeline using Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [113] [A Mean Curvature Approach to Boundary Detection: Geometric Insights for Unsupervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04274)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平均曲率的边界检测框架MCBP，通过离散近似形状算子计算数据流形的点态平均曲率，实现高维数据中聚类边界、异常点和过渡点的统一几何检测，并进一步提出曲率驱动的数据分解方法以增强下游聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据中的精确边界检测在无监督学习中仍是一大挑战，特别是在存在非线性结构和异质密度的情况下。传统基于密度的方法难以应对这些复杂场景，亟需一种不依赖密度参数、能刻画数据流形内在几何结构的边界检测方法。

Method: MCBP框架：(1) 基于局部k近邻块离散近似形状算子，计算点态平均曲率，无需显式流形参数化；(2) 利用平均曲率作为边界结构的几何描述符，高曲率区域对应聚类过渡、几何不规则及低密度界面；(3) 引入自适应百分位数阈值方案实现多尺度边界提取；(4) 提出曲率驱动数据分解，将样本分为平滑子集（低曲率）和边界子集（高曲率），作为非线性几何过滤机制。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，MCBP在复杂和高维场景下显著提升了聚类性能。

Conclusion: MCBP是几何机器学习的具体贡献，展示了曲率感知分析作为连接微分几何与数据驱动建模的统一范式的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate boundary detection in high-dimensional data remains a central challenge in unsupervised learning, particularly in the presence of non-linear structures and heterogeneous densities. In this work, we introduce Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP), a novel geometric framework grounded in Geometric Machine Learning that departs from traditional density-based approaches by explicitly modeling the intrinsic curvature of the data manifold. The method relies on a discrete approximation of the shape operator, estimated from local k-nearest neighbor patches, to compute pointwise mean curvature without requiring explicit manifold parametrization. The key insight of MCBP is to use mean curvature as a principled descriptor of boundary structure: high-curvature regions naturally correspond to transitions between clusters, geometric irregularities, and low-density interfaces. This yields a unified geometric interpretation of boundary, outlier, and transition points. We further introduce an adaptive percentile-based thresholding scheme that enables multiscale boundary extraction without relying on ad hoc density parameters. Beyond detection, we propose a curvature-driven data decomposition that separates samples into smooth (low-curvature) and boundary (high-curvature) subsets, effectively acting as a non-linear geometric filtering mechanism. This representation enhances cluster separability and improves the robustness of downstream unsupervised algorithms. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that MCBP consistently improves clustering performance, particularly in complex and high-dimensional scenarios. These results position MCBP as a concrete contribution to Geometric Machine Learning, highlighting the potential of curvature-aware analysis as a unifying paradigm bridging differential geometry and data-driven modeling.

</details>


### [114] [Gradient Flow Structure and Quantitative Dynamics of Multi-Head Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04279)
*Ayan Pendharkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了多头自注意力动力学的理论框架，解决了若干开放问题。在适当条件下证明了多头能量泛函的非减性，识别了每头单调性的关键障碍——径向阴影项，并给出了确保单调性的充分条件。在简化标量头机制下，推导了控制聚类行为的临界逆温闭式表达式，证明了异质头的超可加聚类速率，以及ReLU与softmax注意力在聚类时间上的分离。最后建立了熵产生恒等式，表明注意力熵随聚类过程单调增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力中不同头之间的几何干扰使标准单调性论证失效，现有工作主要基于单头注意力，多头机制的理论理解尚不充分。本文试图建立多头自注意力动力学的统一理论框架，解决能量单调性、聚类行为等开放问题。

Method: 利用能量泛函分析和动力系统方法，研究多头注意力在单位球面上的梯度流行为。引入径向阴影项概念解释每头单调性失败的原因，提出近似正交性下的鲁棒性条件。在标量头机制和等角token配置下进行解析推导，结合线性化动力学分析聚类时间。

Result: 1) 证明了在合适得分矩阵条件下多头能量泛函沿平坦和球形动力学非减；2) 识别了径向阴影项是每头单调性的关键障碍；3) 给出了确保单调性的充分条件并建立了对近似正交的鲁棒性；4) 推导了临界逆温闭式表达式；5) 证明了异质头的超可加聚类速率；6) 证明了ReLU与softmax注意力在聚类时间上的分离；7) 建立了熵产生恒等式，证明了注意力熵单调增加。

Conclusion: 本文为多头注意力动力学提供了统一视角，阐明了Transformer模型中聚类与稳定性的潜在机制，为理论理解多头注意力提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Transformer self-attention can be interpreted as a gradient flow on the unit sphere, in which tokens evolve under softmax interaction potentials and tend to form clusters. While prior work has established clustering behavior for single-head attention, the multi-head setting remains less understood due to geometric interference between heads, which invalidates standard monotonicity arguments.
  In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for multi-head self-attention dynamics and resolve several open questions. We show that, under suitable conditions on the score matrices, a natural multi-head energy functional is non-decreasing along both flat and spherical dynamics. We identify the key obstruction to per-head monotonicity as radial shadow terms, which are projections of each head's output onto token directions, persisting even under orthogonality assumptions. We introduce a sufficient condition ensuring monotonicity and establish robustness to approximate orthogonality.
  In a simplified scalar-head regime with equiangular token configurations, we derive a closed-form expression for the critical inverse temperature governing clustering behavior, and show that heterogeneous heads exhibit super-additive clustering rates. In this regime, we also prove a separation in clustering time between ReLU and softmax attention in the linearized dynamics. Finally, we establish an entropy production identity and show that attention entropy increases monotonically toward equilibrium as clustering progresses.
  Our results provide a unified perspective on the dynamics of multi-head attention and clarify the mechanisms underlying clustering and stability in transformer models.

</details>


### [115] [Leveraging Pretrained Language Models as Energy Functions for Glauber Dynamics Text Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04291)
*Tarun Kathuria,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Glauber动力学的离散扩散语言模型，利用预训练因果/掩码语言模型构建能量函数作为平稳分布，显著提升文本生成质量。结合UL2模型后，在多个任务上优于此前扩散模型，与同等规模自回归模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型采用均匀转移核作为前向过程，文本生成质量受限。作者希望利用预训练语言模型（如因果/掩码LM）构建能量函数，将其作为平稳分布来指导Glauber动力学采样，从而提升离散扩散模型的生成质量。

Method: 基于Glauber动力学构建离散扩散模型：不采用传统均匀转移核的前向扩散过程，而是利用预训练的因果/掩码语言模型定义能量函数，将该能量函数对应的平稳分布作为采样目标，通过Glauber动力学进行反向生成。采用UL2作为预训练模型整合到扩散管线中。

Result: 在文本生成质量上优于此前扩散语言模型，与同等规模自回归模型性能相当；在零样本常识推理、Sudoku和Zebra谜题等规划与搜索任务上，优于此前扩散模型及GPT-2风格的自回归模型。

Conclusion: 基于能量函数的离散扩散框架有效提升了文本生成质量，展示了预训练LM作为能量函数在扩散模型中的潜力，在多种任务上达到或超越自回归模型水平。

Abstract: We present a discrete diffusion-based language model using Glauber dynamics from statistical physics. Our main insight is that instead of trying to train a discrete state space diffusion model using Glauber dynamics with a uniform transition kernel as the forward process, one can set up an ``energy function'' based on pretrained causal/masked language models. When viewed as the stationary distribution, this energy function allows us to significantly improve the quality of the generated text. Incorporating UL2 as the pretrained model into our diffusion pipeline, we outperform prior diffusion based LMs and perform competitively with autoregressive models of comparable model sizes. Furthermore, our models are competitive with or outperform prior diffusion models and GPT-2 style auto-regressive models on zero-shot common sense reasoning tasks as well as planning and search tasks like Sudoku and Zebra puzzles.

</details>


### [116] [LLMs Uncertainty Quantification via Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04295)
*Hamed Karimi,Vaishali Meyappan,Reza Samavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应共形语义熵（ACSE）方法，通过聚类LLM输出的语义熵来量化不确定性，并利用共形校准提供统计保证，在多个数据集上显著优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在产生幻觉时表现出过度自信，这在安全关键场景中构成挑战。现有不确定性量化方法多关注词法或概率度量，忽略了语义相似但表达不同的响应之间的语义方差。

Method: 提出ACSE方法，通过对同一提示的多个不同响应进行语义熵聚类，自适应调整不确定性评分；采用共形校准提供有限样本、分布无关的统计保证，以用户指定误差率对提示进行接受/弃权决策。

Result: 在多个LLM和数据集上的实验表明，ACSE在判别性能、共形保证和概率校准指标上均一致优于现有不确定性量化基线。例如在TriviaQA上AUROC达到0.88，而词元熵方法仅为0.65。

Conclusion: ACSE通过语义感知的自适应不确定性量化结合共形校准，有效解决了LLM过度自信问题，为安全关键部署提供了可靠的不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: LLMs' overconfidence, particularly when hallucinating, poses a significant challenge for the deployment of the models in safety-critical settings and makes a reliable estimation of uncertainty necessary. Existing approaches for uncertainty quantification typically prioritize lexical or probabilistic measures; however, these techniques often ignore the semantic variance of different responses with similar meaning. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy (ACSE), a method for estimating prompt-level uncertainty by adaptively measuring semantic dispersion in LLMs outputs. Our uncertainty scoring function is based on clustering semantic entropy of multiple diverse responses to the same prompt. The function adaptively adjusts the uncertainty score based on semantic features of each cluster. To ensure statistical reliability of our score, we use conformal calibration to apply a decision rule to accept/abstain the prompts, providing a finite-sample, distribution-free guarantee such that the error rate among the accepted responses remains bounded by a user-specified tolerance. Our extensive experimental evaluations using different LLMs and datasets, demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art uncertainty quantification baselines using discriminative performance, conformal guarantees, and probabilistic calibration indicators. As a highlight, for TriviaQA dataset, AUROC of our approach is 0.88 compared to 0.65 produced by the token entropy approach.

</details>


### [117] [Memory as a Markov Matrix: Sample Efficient Knowledge Expansion via Token-to-Dictionary Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04308)
*Kaustubh Pethkar,Ziyang Xiong,Zuofeng Shang,Yingcong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LLM持续学习建模为马尔可夫链，通过状态空间扩展和嵌入调优实现零遗忘的新知识注入，并给出了样本复杂度的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法存在两个根本局限：1）遗忘不可避免；2）模型更新往往是不可逆的。随着LLM表达能力的增强，需要质疑大规模权重更新对于获取少量新知识的必要性。

Method: 提出将自回归语言生成建模为马尔可夫过程，模型记忆由马尔可夫转移矩阵表示；新知识/词元的注入对应于状态空间扩展，保留现有转移即可保证已有知识的记忆。通过词元到词典的映射策略，理论上证明了注入新词元的样本复杂度与它所映射到的已有词元数量呈线性关系。为实现该映射，提出了嵌入调优算法，只需极少的参数更新。

Result: 1）建立了马尔可夫过程下的语言生成形式化框架；2）证明了新词元注入的样本复杂度界限；3）提出了嵌入调优算法，实现零遗忘和极小参数更新；4）实验验证了方法的有效性和理论结论的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种将自回归语言生成建模为马尔可夫过程的框架，通过状态空间扩展和嵌入调优实现新知识注入，在理论上证明了样本复杂度界限，实验验证了该方法能实现零遗忘且只需极少的参数更新。

Abstract: Continual incorporation of new knowledge is essential for the long-term evolution of large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches typically rely on parameter-update algorithms to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, yet they suffer from fundamental limitations: 1) forgetting is unavoidable as the amount of newly injected knowledge grows; and 2) model updates are often irreversible. As modern LLMs become increasingly expressive, it is natural to question whether large-scale weight updates are necessary for acquiring a small amount of new knowledge. In this work, we propose a principled framework that models autoregressive language generation as a Markov process over tokens, where model memory is represented by a Markov transition matrix. Under this formulation, incorporating new knowledge/tokens corresponds to extending the state space, and preserving existing transitions guarantees retention of previously learned knowledge. We then prove a sample complexity bound for incorporating new tokens via a token-to-dictionary mapping strategy. In particular, for learning the transition behavior of each new token, the required number of samples scales linearly with the number of existing tokens it is mapped to. To realize this mapping, we propose an embedding-tuning algorithm that requires minimal parameter updates and induces zero forgetting. Experimental results further demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [118] [LUCAS-MEGA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Representation Learning in Soil-Environment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04323)
*Kuangdai Leng,Simon Jeffery,Panos Panagos,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LUCAS-MEGA，一个大规模多模态土壤数据集（7万+样本，1000+特征），以及用于数据融合的SoilFuser流水线和基于多模态表格Transformer的SoilFormer预训练模型，实现了高维土壤表征学习与不确定性感知预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土壤研究受限于碎片化、异构的数据集，只能进行小规模预测建模，无法支持高维表征学习，亟需大规模、多模态、统一格式的数据基础设施。

Method: 1) 以LUCAS调查数据为骨干，融合68个来源数据集构建LUCAS-MEGA；2) 开发SoilFuser多智能体+人在回路的数据融合流水线，标准化异构格式、解决不一致和错误、整合自然语言注释；3) 基于LUCAS-MEGA预训练多模态表格Transformer (SoilFormer)，采用自监督特征掩码目标。

Result: SoilFormer实现稳定训练、强预测性能、不确定性感知表征，且学到的表征与已知土壤过程一致。数据集通过开放性API发布。

Conclusion: LUCAS-MEGA解决了土壤数据碎片化瓶颈，使高维表征学习成为可能，为农业、碳循环和环境可持续性研究提供了可扩展的数据基础。

Abstract: Understanding soil is fundamental to agriculture, carbon cycling, and environmental sustainability, yet progress is limited by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets that constrain modeling to small-scale predictive settings rather than high-dimensional representation learning. We introduce LUCAS-MEGA, a large-scale multimodal dataset constructed through systematic data fusion of European soil-environment observations, with the LUCAS survey as its backbone. The fused dataset comprises over 70,000 samples and more than 1,000 features spanning physical, chemical, environmental, biological, and visual attributes, aggregated from 68 source datasets. To enable integration at scale, we develop SoilFuser, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop data fusion pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous data formats and measurement protocols, resolves inconsistencies and invalid entries (e.g., unit inconsistencies, codebook mismatches, and erroneous values), incorporates natural language annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes and metadata into a unified, machine learning-ready feature space. The resulting dataset captures key characteristics of real-world soil observations, including multimodality, uneven feature coverage, and heterogeneous uncertainty. To demonstrate the usability of LUCAS-MEGA for data-driven modeling, we pretrain a multimodal tabular transformer (SoilFormer) using a self-supervised objective based on feature masking, achieving stable training, strong predictive performance, and representations that support uncertainty-aware prediction. We further show that the learned representations recover relationships consistent with established soil processes. LUCAS-MEGA is released with open access and is accompanied by composable, agent-friendly APIs that support structured querying and data-driven workflows.

</details>


### [119] [DeFed-GMM-DaDiL: A Decentralized Federated Framework for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04324)
*Rebecca Clain,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Fred Ngole Mboula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DeFed-GMM-DaDiL的完全去中心化联邦学习方法，通过GMM和Wasserstein重心实现多源域适应，无需中心服务器即可保护隐私并处理目标域缺失类别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决去中心化环境下多源域适应问题，在保护各客户端数据隐私的同时，实现从多个异构源域到无标签目标域的知识迁移，并应对目标域类别缺失的挑战。

Method: 采用完全去中心化的联邦方法DeFed-GMM-DaDiL，扩展了GMM-字典学习框架。每个客户端将数据集建模为高斯混合模型（GMM），联邦整体通过带标签的Wasserstein重心联合逼近共享的可学习GMM原子，无需中心服务器即可实现适应。

Result: 在目标域存在缺失类别的情况下，DeFed-GMM-DaDiL能够保持稳定一致的跨客户端共享表示，有效重构缺失类别，并在多源域适应基准测试中取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL成功实现了去中心化多源域适应，兼顾了隐私保护、表示稳定性与类别重构能力，性能具竞争力。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-source domain adaptation seeks to transfer knowledge from multiple heterogeneous and related source domains to an unlabeled target domain in a decentralized setting. We address this challenge through a fully decentralized federated approach, DeFed-GMM-DaDiL, an extension of the GMM-Dataset Dictionary Learning (DaDiL) framework. Each client models its dataset as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and the federation jointly approximates them via labeled Wasserstein barycenters of shared, learnable GMM atoms. This design enables adaptation without a central server while preserving clients' privacy. We empirically study the stability of the learned representations in scenarios where the target domain has missing classes. Empirical results demonstrate that DeFed-GMM-DaDiL maintains stable and consistent shared representations across clients, effectively reconstructs missing classes, and achieves competitive performance on multi-source domain adaptation benchmarks.

</details>


### [120] [On the Architectural Complexity of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04325)
*Nicholas J. Cooper,François G. Meyer,Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata-Carratalá,Lijun Chen,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的DNN理论框架，通过显式建模张量操作结构，实现了对40年来架构复杂性的分析，并自动发现了3000多种未被探索的高复杂度架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN理论在抽象化张量操作的低层信息方面存在空白，缺乏一个能同时分析架构复杂性演化并自动构建新架构的统一理论框架。

Method: 提出统一理论框架显式建模张量操作结构；分析40年间DNN架构的复杂性演化；基于框架识别高复杂度架构类别；构建包含3000+架构的数据集。

Result: 揭示了突破性架构与不同种类架构复杂性增加之间的关联；识别出多个尚未被探索的高复杂度架构类别；收集并公开了3000+高复杂度架构数据集。

Conclusion: 该理论框架不仅能分析DNN架构复杂性的历史演化规律，还能系统性地发现和构建新的高复杂度架构，为自动架构设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a unified theoretical framework for the rigorous analysis and systematic construction of deep neural networks (DNNs). This framework addresses a gap in existing theory by explicitly modeling the structure of tensor operations -- lower level information that is often abstracted. Our framework enables two novel objectives: (1) analysis of the evolution of architectural complexity over deep learning history, and (2) automatic construction of novel architectures based on new types of tensor operations. Our study of DNNs introduced over the past 40 years reveals a connection between groundbreaking architectures and increases in different types of architectural complexity. Moreover, we identify several large classes of higher complexity architectures that have not yet been explored. We then collect a dataset of 3,000+ higher complexity architectures, which we publicly release at: https://github.com/combinatoriallabs/ArchitecturalComplexity.

</details>


### [121] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的统计精化方法，在未知噪声分布、无干净图像或校准数据的情况下，通过贝叶斯辅助信号框架提升给定去噪器的结果质量与噪声统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法通常需要精确的图像/噪声建模或干净数据，但在实际场景中，噪声分布未知且无干净样本可用，导致去噪结果与噪声统计特性不一致，效果欠佳。

Method: 基于条件像素独立噪声假设，利用贝叶斯公式构建辅助信号，从含噪数据中提取统计信息，评估并优化去噪结果与噪声统计量之间的一致性。

Result: 该方法无需噪声分布精确信息或干净图像/校准数据，即可增强去噪结果的统计一致性并提升去噪质量。

Conclusion: 利用含噪数据中的统计信息可以有效地改善任意去噪器在缺乏先验知识条件下的性能，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

</details>


### [122] [Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04363)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,SoonYoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN在标签偏移下容易过拟合多数类，本文提出DistPFN——首个针对表格基础模型的后验调整方法，通过重新缩放预测概率来降低训练先验的影响并增强后验贡献，DistPFN-T进一步引入温度缩放自适应调整，在250+OpenML数据集上证实了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN虽然在表格数据上表现优异，但存在对标签偏移的脆弱性，即当训练集中存在类别不平衡时，模型会过度拟合多数类，影响分类性能。

Method: 提出DistPFN，一种测试时后验调整方法，通过重新缩放预测概率，降低上下文先验（训练集类别分布）的影响，增强模型预测后验的贡献；并进一步提出DistPFN-T，引入温度缩放自适应控制调整强度。

Result: 在250多个OpenML数据集上评估，DistPFN和DistPFN-T在标签偏移下显著提升了TabPFN系列模型的分类性能，同时在无标签偏移的标准设置下保持强性能。

Conclusion: DistPFN有效解决了TabPFN在标签偏移下的过拟合问题，无需修改架构或额外训练，是一种轻量级且有效的测试时后验调整方法。

Abstract: TabPFN has recently gained attention as a foundation model for tabular datasets, achieving strong performance by leveraging in-context learning on synthetic data. However, we find that TabPFN is vulnerable to label shift, often overfitting to the majority class in the training dataset. To address this limitation, we propose DistPFN, the first test-time posterior adjustment method designed for tabular foundation models. DistPFN rescales predicted class probabilities by downweighting the influence of the training prior (i.e., the class distribution of the context) and emphasizing the contribution of the model's predicted posterior, without architectural modification or additional training. We further introduce DistPFN-T, which incorporates temperature scaling to adaptively control the adjustment strength based on the discrepancy between prior and posterior. We evaluate our methods on over 250 OpenML datasets, demonstrating substantial improvements for various TabPFN-based models in classification tasks under label shift, while maintaining strong performance in standard settings without label shift. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/seunghan96/DistPFN.

</details>


### [123] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文正式建立了部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟与动作延迟之间的等价性，证明两者在Dec-POMDP框架下生成相同的联合策略集和轨迹分布，并将单智能体结果推广到任意时域的多智能体问题。同时发现，尽管最优解空间结构同构，实际学习动态存在本质差异，并利用结构等价性实现了零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅建立了单智能体无限时域下观测延迟与动作延迟的等价性，缺乏对多智能体系统、任意时域以及混合延迟配置的通用理论分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，并为延迟场景中的多智能体强化学习提供理论基础。

Method: 采用形式化证明方法建立观测延迟与动作延迟在Dec-POMDP中的等价性，通过观察-动作历史证明两者生成相同的联合策略集和稳态轨迹分布。在TI-MDP中进一步证明可化简为最小局部增广状态。通过数值实验对比两种延迟下的学习动态差异，并基于结构等价性实现零样本策略迁移。

Result: 1) 正式证明了任意时域部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟与动作延迟的等价性；2) 证明了TI-MDP中观察-动作历史可简化为最小局部增广状态；3) 发现尽管解空间同构，TD算法中的因果信用分配错误导致学习动态存在本质差异；4) 利用结构等价性成功实现了从动作延迟到观测延迟的零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 观测延迟与动作延迟在Dec-POMDP中具有结构等价性，但实际学习动态受因果信用分配问题影响而不同。该等价性可被利用来绕过学习挑战，实现跨延迟类型的策略迁移，为复杂延迟系统中的统一高效求解方法奠定基础。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [124] [Extending Differential Temporal Difference Methods for Episodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04368)
*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的差分时序差分方法，将奖励中心化技术应用于 episodic 问题，并通过理论证明和实验验证该方法能保持策略排序、继承线性TD的理论保证，并提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分TD方法依赖于奖励中心化，但该方法在episodic问题中会改变最优策略，限制了其应用。受深度强化学习中归一化作用的启发，研究者希望将奖励中心化推广到episodic问题中。

Method: 提出差分TD的泛化形式，证明其在存在终止条件的情况下保持策略排序；证明与线性TD的等价性，从而继承理论保证；将多种流式强化学习算法扩展为对应的差分版本。

Result: 在多种基算法和环境上的实验表明，奖励中心化能够提升episodic问题的样本效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的泛化差分TD方法成功将奖励中心化扩展到episodic问题，保留了理论优势并提升了实际性能。

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [125] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer在组合泛化任务中，其“记忆vs推理”的结局是由训练过程中一个狭窄且可识别的关键窗口决定的。在该窗口内施加权重衰减（即使只占整个训练的25%）能显著提升OOD准确率，窗口位置的微小偏移会导致性能从随机跳跃到推理水平。这一现象在小初始化下推理吸引域反而收缩，且该现象具有任务特异性（在模算术的“grokking”任务中不出现）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的分析将复杂度控制视为单一静态超参数，不清楚训练过程中何时其实起决定性作用。本文旨在揭示“记忆vs推理”命运在训练中的具体时间窗口。

Method: 在受控组合泛化任务上，通过系统性地在不同训练阶段施加权重衰减等正则化手段，观察其对OOD准确率的影响，并分析窗口位置与初始化尺度的关系。

Result: （i）仅25%训练窗口的权重衰减即可达到全训练权重衰减的OOD准确率（0.93 vs 0.91）；（ii）固定正则化预算下，窗口置于训练中期比早期OOD准确率高5-9倍；（iii）窗口边界极尖锐，100步偏移导致OOD从0.15跃至0.61；（iv）窗口位置依赖于初始化尺度，但小初始化下推理吸引域反而收缩。

Conclusion: Transformer的组合泛化存在一个决定性的关键训练窗口，正则化在该窗口内施加最为有效，且该现象具有任务特异性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [126] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本工作构建了一个基于SL(3, Z)子群构造问题的数学推理基准套件，利用密码学风格的验证者-证明者信息不对称性，评估语言模型在结构数学推理中对开放可判定性边界问题的处理能力。实验发现，某前沿模型在索引变体上经过152分钟推理后，因识别出核侧成员判定问题为计算瓶颈而主动选择"不知道"而非给出错误承诺，展示了校准的元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准多依赖标准答案评分，无法区分模型的"正确承诺"与"正确弃权"行为，尤其缺乏对开放可判定性边界上模型元认知能力的系统性评估。

Method: 基于SL(3, Z)中有限生成子群构造问题，设计三类算术不变量（指数、素数满射、成员判定）；利用构造信息(N, K)可在O(1)闭式求解而求解者因信息缺失需借助Aschbacher分类分析或未知可判定性的成员查询这一不对称性，构建验证者-证明者博弈。

Result: 在两个最先进模型的五种代表性推理轨迹上，索引变体实例中一个模型花费152分钟推理，明确识别核侧成员判定问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证后以"DON'T KNOW"弃权而非提交计算的余核候选，展现了在开放可判定性边界上的校准元认知。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了标准答案评分所混淆的四类行为（正确承诺、错误承诺、正确弃权、错误弃权），为评估语言模型在结构性数学推理中的元认知校准能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [127] [Demystifying Manifold Constraints in LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04418)
*Kang An,Jiaxiang Li,Donald Goldfarb,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACRO优化框架，通过显式流形约束替代启发式正则化技术（如归一化层和权重衰减），在保证理论收敛性的同时实现与大语言模型预训练相当的性能，并揭示了约束机制独立绑定前向激活尺度与稳定旋转平衡的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预训练依赖启发式稳定技术（如归一化层、权重衰减），但这些方法的机制和动机尚未明确，且缺乏理论保障。本文旨在系统揭示显式流形约束在LLM预训练中的作用。

Method: 提出MACRO（Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer），一个可证明收敛的单循环优化框架，通过显式流形约束将权重正则化启发式与RMS归一化、解耦权重衰减等交互机制解耦。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明，流形约束能够独立绑定前向激活尺度并强制稳定旋转平衡，从而取代启发式机制的作用。在大规模LLM架构上的评估显示MACRO实现了极具竞争力的性能，同时保持了精确黎曼优化的理论保证。

Conclusion: 显式流形约束可以系统地替代LLM预训练中的启发式稳定技术（如归一化层和权重衰减），在保持理论严格性的同时达到同等甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: The empirical success of large language model (LLM) pre-training relies heavily on heuristic stabilization techniques, such as explicit normalization layers and weight decay. While recent constrained optimization approaches that explicitly restrict weights may improve numerical stability and performance, the mechanism and motivation for adding constraints still remain elusive. This paper systematically demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training. By introducing the Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)-a provably convergent, single-loop optimization framework-our study disentangles weight regularization heuristics from interacting mechanisms like RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay. Theoretical analyses and comprehensive empirical evaluations reveal that manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, thereby subsuming the roles of these heuristic mechanisms. Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [128] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对边际稳定、部分可观的线性动力系统在非随机扰动下的在线预测问题，提出了一种利用预测性提示（predictive hints）稳定学习过程的非约束在线最小二乘法，在有模型知识时实现对数遗憾，在无模型知识下对对称系统同样保持对数遗憾，实现了优于经典固定增益观测器的自适应最优在线预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边际稳定系统中轨迹可能无界增长，标准在线学习方法依赖于有界域/梯度假设，其保证在无界轨迹场景下失效。因此需要一种能够处理无界轨迹的在线预测方法。

Method: 提出非约束在线最小二乘法，利用预测性提示（predictive hints）稳定学习过程。在有模型知识时，使用任意稳定Luenberger观测器构造提示使残差一致有界；在无模型知识的对称系统中，引入通用提示同样实现对数遗憾。

Result: 在有模型知识下实现了对数遗憾；在无模型知识的对称系统中同样保持对数遗憾；相较于经典固定增益观测器，在非随机扰动下实现了自适应实例最优的在线预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效解决了边际稳定系统中在线预测的挑战，在不同知识水平下均能实现对数遗憾，为动态系统在线预测提供了理论保证和实用方案。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [129] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出p进流形学习，给出相应算法，并基于公平博弈设计基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流形学习基于实数域，但某些数据（如博弈树、决策过程）具有p进数结构，现有方法难以建模其层次性与非阿基米德几何特性。

Method: 提出p进流形学习框架，设计适配p进数域的降维或嵌入算法，并利用公平博弈（如Nim游戏）构建具有p进拓扑结构的基准数据集。

Result: 算法能有效捕捉p进数空间中的层次结构，基准任务可验证模型对非欧几里得结构的表征能力。

Conclusion: p进流形学习为处理具有p进数结构的数据提供了新范式，公平博弈基准有助于推动该方向研究。

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [130] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），在放弃全局单调性假设后，通过机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输条件实现完全反事实可识别，并在机器人交互任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型的反事实可识别性大多依赖全局单调性等严格假设，但在具身交互场景中，同一外生扰动在不同接触上下文中可能诱发相反响应，全局单调性常被违反。作者希望探索在放弃全局单调性后，何种结构仍能保证反事实的可识别性。

Method: 引入非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），保留三角递归结构但用机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性，证明其等价于外生同构并蕴含完全反事实可识别性。实例化为CausalInverter模型，包含三角可逆层、方向门控和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 在合成非单调机制上，结构偏差随非单调性增强带来系统性反事实增益；在MuJoCo Door任务中实现完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线，且恢复稳定性显著优于Transformer和条件流预测器；在非单调性较弱的MuJoCo Push任务中，低数据预测器仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 识别出全局单调三角模型与无约束黑箱世界模型之间的更广泛可识别区域，为具身交互中的反事实推理提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [131] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从交通调查数据中学习稀疏、可解释、政策可行的群体干预策略的框架，通过非负潜在表征、Shapley归因和熵正则化最优传输，实现目标群体向参考群体的分布对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通调查分析多为描述性或预测性，缺乏能生成稀疏、政策可行的干预策略的方法。

Method: 1) 固定基非负潜在表征保持干预前后可比性；2) Shapley归因识别目标相关潜在因子并映射到可控变量；3) 熵正则化最优传输最小化干预后目标分布与参考分布的差异；4) 加权ℓ₂,₁正则化促进政策杠杆的稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上，所提框架能生成紧凑、可解释的政策可行干预方案，具有明确的调整幅度，提升了群体转化率并保持了干预稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥补了从调查数据到可行政策干预的鸿沟，为交通政策制定提供了数据驱动的稀疏干预策略生成工具。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies. We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [132] [A Queueing-Theoretic Framework for Stability Analysis of LLM Inference with KV Cache Memory Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04595)
*Chengyi Nie,Nian Si,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个将计算和GPU内存约束同时纳入LLM推理分析的排队论框架，推导了严格的稳定性和不稳定性条件，并通过实际GPU生产环境实验验证了预测的准确性（偏差通常在10%以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理面临计算和KV缓存内存双重瓶颈，现有工作缺乏统一的理论框架来指导GPU资源部署，导致过度采购或性能违规问题。

Method: 建立排队论框架，显式建模计算约束和GPU内存约束；基于该框架推导稳定/不稳定条件；结合到达率与稳定服务率计算所需集群规模。

Result: 在实际GPU生产环境中的实验验证表明，预测的稳定条件高度准确，偏差通常不超过10%。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM推理服务的GPU资源配置提供了理论依据，能够有效避免过度采购和欠配置问题，解决了GPU供应规划的核心挑战。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has created significant challenges for efficient inference at scale. Unlike traditional workloads, LLM inference is constrained by both computation and the memory overhead of key-value (KV) caching, which accelerates decoding but quickly exhausts GPU memory. In this paper, we introduce the first queueing-theoretic framework that explicitly incorporates both computation and GPU memory constraints into the analysis of LLM inference. Based on this framework, we derive rigorous stability and instability conditions that determine whether an LLM inference service can sustain incoming demand without unbounded queue growth. This result offers a powerful tool for system deployment, potentially addressing the core challenge of GPU provisioning. By combining an estimated request arrival rate with our derived stable service rate, operators can calculate the necessary cluster size to avoid both costly over-purchasing and performance-violating under-provisioning. We further validate our theoretical predictions through extensive experiments in real GPU production environments. Our results show that the predicted stability conditions are highly accurate, with deviations typically within 10%.

</details>


### [133] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WZ-LLM神经符号框架，将Wilf-Zeilberger方法与LLM证明器结合，在Lean 4中实现组合恒等式的自动化形式证明，在LCI-Test上达到34%证明成功率，优于DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化组合恒等式的形式证明对基于LLM的证明器具有挑战性，因为需要长程证明规划且无约束搜索会迅速爆炸。符号方法如WZ方法可以通过构造辅助函数和递推关系实现机械化证明，但需要有效集成到LLM框架中。

Method: 提出WZ-LLM神经符号框架：(1) 将WZ证明计划转化为Lean 4中可执行的证明草图；(2) 使用基于LLM的证明器完成机器可验证的子目标；(3) 通过Lean内核验证的引导循环训练专用WZ-Prover，包含专家验证迭代和DAPO强化学习优化。

Result: 在LCI-Test(100个经典组合恒等式)上达到34%证明成功率，超越DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等强基线；在CombiBench和PutnamBench-Comb上持续取得增益。

Conclusion: 框架提供了两种互补优势：一是对超出WZ范围的恒等式改进了直接证明能力，二是当WZ草图引导专用证明器时大幅提升了端到端成功率。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [134] [Library learning with e-graphs on jazz harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04622)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers,Xinyi Guan,Martin Rohrmeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于库学习的计算模型，用于学习爵士乐和声模式。该模型通过枚举每首曲子的可能程序，然后联合学习和声模式库和重构程序，利用演绎解析与e-graph上的库学习来高效搜索程序与库的联合空间。通过评估程序与库的直观性以及与人工编写和声推导的相似性，验证模型对人类音乐模式学习能力的捕捉效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够获得高度结构化的音乐模式直觉理解，但这些模式通常需要多次反复聆听和反思才能完全内化。作者希望捕捉这种内化过程，通过计算模型来模拟人类如何学习和声模式。

Method: 基于库学习的计算模型。给定和声进行语料库，模型搜索由基本和声关系组成的程序空间，以发现语料库的简洁生成式解释。首先枚举每首曲子的可能程序，然后联合学习和声模式库和重构程序。为高效搜索程序和库的联合空间，将演绎解析与e-graph上的库学习相结合。

Result: 通过评估程序和库的直观性以及与人工编写和声推导的相似性，验证了模型对人类音乐模式学习能力的捕捉效果。

Conclusion: 该计算模型能够通过库学习机制有效捕捉人类学习和声模式的内化过程，在直观性和与人类推导的相似性方面表现良好。

Abstract: Humans can acquire a highly structured intuitive understanding of musical patterns, yet these patterns often require multiple iterations of reflection and re-listening to internalize fully. To capture such an internalization process, we present a computational model for the learning of jazz harmonic patterns based on library learning. Given a corpus of harmonic progressions, our model searches over a space of programs composed of primitive harmonic relations in order to discover concise generative explanations of the corpus. The model first enumerates possible programs for each piece, and then jointly learns a library of harmonic patterns and refactored programs. To efficiently navigate the vast joint space of programs and libraries, we integrate deductive parsing with library learning on e-graphs. We explore how well our model captures aspects of human musical pattern learning by evaluating the intuitiveness of both programs and libraries, as well as similarities to human-written harmonic derivations.

</details>


### [135] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEPO方法，通过利用历史数据构建不确定性奖励，实现在线RLHF中高效探索，提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLHF中现有探索策略依赖on-policy期望估计，难以从有限的历史偏好数据中可靠估计，导致策略过早忽略未充分探索但可能包含高价值行为的区域，亟需更高效的探索方法。

Method: 提出数据依赖的偏好优化（DEPO），利用历史数据为高不确定性区域构建额外不确定性奖励，引导模型探索潜在高价值数据点。

Result: 理论上推导了数据依赖的遗憾界，表明算法能自适应学习任务的难度，比最坏情况界更紧；实验上在多个基准测试中一致优于强基线方法，样本效率显著提升。

Conclusion: DEPO是一种简单可扩展的在线RLHF探索方法，通过数据驱动的探索奖励有效提升样本效率，理论分析和实验验证均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [136] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quadrature-TreeSHAP，一种基于高斯-勒让德求积法的路径依赖 TreeSHAP 重表述，使用仅8个固定求积点即可达到机器精度，在数值稳定性、深度无关性和高阶交互支持方面优于现有方法，CPU和GPU上均实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有树集成模型的 Shapley 值解释方法（如 Path-Dependent TreeSHAP）在计算深度依赖的运行时间、数值稳定性以及高阶交互支持之间存在权衡，亟需一种同时解决这些问题的统一方法。

Method: 基于加权 Banzhaf 交互多项式将 Banzhaf 交互值表达为特征参与概率 p 下的期望，通过高斯-勒让德求积法在 p ∈ [0,1] 上积分恢复 Shapley 值及任意阶交互值，仅需 8 个固定求积点即可达到机器精度，消除深度依赖并支持 SIMD 高效执行。

Result: 在 12 个 XGBoost 基准上，CPU 加速 1.06x-10.59x（Shapley 值）、3.80x-58.11x（成对交互）；GPU 加速 1.84x-6.95x；高阶交互相比 TreeSHAP-IQ 最高加速 1200x，且数值稳定性优于 TreeSHAP。

Conclusion: Quadrature-TreeSHAP 通过求积法框架统一解决了现有方法在数值稳定性、深度依赖性及高阶交互支持上的 trade-off，是一种高效、稳定且可扩展的树模型解释方法，已集成至 XGBoost。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [137] [Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement for Incentivizing Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04747)
*Leon Witt,Togrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Wojciech Samek,Lucy Klinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识无关相关性协议（KFCA），用于联邦学习中无需真实标签、公开测试集或分布知识的客户端贡献奖励机制，在分类报告和诚实多数假设下严格诚实，解决了相关性协议（CA）的标签翻转漏洞，并在联邦LLM适配器调优和真实PCB检测任务中验证了高效实时奖励计算，适用于去中心化和基于区块链的激励机制设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中需要一种无需依赖真实标签、公开测试集或先验分布知识的客户端贡献奖励机制，同时解决现有相关性协议（CA）存在的标签翻转漏洞问题。

Method: 提出知识无关相关性协议（KFCA），在分类报告和诚实多数假设下实现严格诚实性，通过消除对真实标签的依赖来防御标签翻转攻击。

Result: 在联邦大语言模型适配器调优和真实PCB（印刷电路板）检测任务上验证了KFCA的有效性，展示了高效的实时奖励计算能力。

Conclusion: KFCA适用于去中心化和基于区块链的激励机制设计，为联邦学习提供了一种安全、高效且无需先验知识的客户端贡献评估方案。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA) to reward client contributions in federated learning (FL) without relying on ground truth, a public test set, or distribution knowledge. Under categorical reports and an honest majority, KFCA is strictly truthful, addressing the label-flipping vulnerability of Correlated Agreement (CA). We evaluate KFCA on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, showing efficient real-time reward computation suitable for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs.

</details>


### [138] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FL-Sailer是首个专为scATAC-seq数据设计的联邦学习框架，通过自适应杠杆评分采样（降维80%）和不变VAE架构（通过互信息最小化解耦生物信号与技术混杂因素）两大创新，解决了超高维度、极端稀疏性和跨机构异质性三大挑战，并提供了收敛保证。实验表明该方法不仅实现多机构协作，还通过自适应采样作为隐式正则化器超越集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scATAC-seq数据具有高分辨率染色质可及性映射能力，但隐私法规和数据规模限制阻碍了多机构数据共享。联邦学习虽提供隐私保护方案，却面临scATAC-seq分析中超高维度、极端稀疏性和跨机构异质性三大根本性障碍，现有FL方法无法直接适用。

Method: FL-Sailer包含两大核心创新：(i) 自适应杠杆评分采样——选择生物学可解释特征，将维度降低80%；(ii) 不变VAE架构——通过互信息最小化将生物信号与技术混杂因素解耦。同时提供了收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在合成和真实表观基因组数据集上的大量实验表明，FL-Sailer不仅实现了先前不可行的多机构协作，还通过自适应采样作为隐式正则化器抑制技术噪声，从而超越了集中式方法的表现。

Conclusion: 当联邦学习针对特定领域挑战进行定制化设计时，可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。该工作验证了领域适配的FL在计算生物学中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [139] [YOTOnet: Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Fault Diagnosis via Domain-Conditioned Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04528)
*Zesen Wang,Zihao Wu,Yue Hu,Yang Gao,Fuzhen Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: YOTOnet是一种专为跨域机械故障诊断设计的架构，通过物理感知不变特征蒸馏器、域条件稀疏专家和双头分类系统实现一次性训练即可部署的零样本泛化，在5个公开轴承数据集上的30个跨数据集协议中表现优越，并展现出清晰的数据规模扩展效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习故障诊断模型在不同设备和工况下存在严重的领域偏移问题，导致泛化能力受限，受基础模型零样本泛化成功的启发，本文旨在设计一种可一次性训练并部署的跨域故障诊断架构。

Method: 提出YOTOnet架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 物理感知不变特征蒸馏器（利用多尺度膨胀卷积和FFT时频融合提取域无关表征）；(2) 域条件稀疏专家（DC-MoE，通过学习门控自适应路由输入到专用处理器，无需外部元数据）；(3) 双头分类系统（带辅助监督）。

Result: 在5个公开轴承数据集（CWRU, MFPT, XJTU, OTTAWA, HUST）上通过30个跨数据集协议验证，YOTOnet优于其他SOTA方法。观察到清晰的数据规模扩展效应：平均测试F1从使用1个训练集时的0.5339提升到使用4个数据集时的0.705。

Conclusion: 实证证据表明基础模型原则（数据规模扩展）能够为工业故障诊断实现鲁棒的、一次性训练即可部署的方案。

Abstract: Mechanical equipment forms the critical backbone of modern industrial production, yet domain shift severely limits the generalization of deep learning based fault diagnosis models across different equipment and operating conditions.Inspired by the success of foundation models in achieving zero-shotgeneralization, we propose YOTOnet (You Only Train Once), a novel architecture specifically designed for cross-domain fault diagnosis in mechanical equipment.YOTOnet comprises three core components: (1) a physics-aware Invariant Feature Distiller that extracts domain-agnostic representations using multi-scale dilated convolutions and FFT-based time-frequency fusion,(2) Domain-Conditioned Sparse Experts (DC-MoE) that adaptively route inputs to specialized processors via learned gating without external meta-data, and (3) a dual-head classification system with auxiliary supervision.Extensive validation on five public bearing datasets (CWRU, MFPT, XJTU,OTTAWA, HUST) through 30 cross-dataset protocols demonstrates the superiority of YOTOnet compared with other state-of-the-art methods. Critically, we observe a clear scaling effect-average test F1 improves from 0.5339(1 training dataset) to 0.705 (4 datasets), with a clear gain when moving from 3 to 4 datasets. These findings provide empirical evidence that foundation model principles can enable robust, train-once deployment for industrial fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [140] [Cognitive Twins: Investigating Personalized Thinking Model Building and Its Performance Enhancement with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04761)
*Wu-Yuin Hwang,Nur Alif Ilyasa,Muhammad Irfan Luthfi,Yuniar Indrihapsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了个性化思维模型（PTM），一种基于Marzano教育目标新分类学的五层分层可解释学习者表征框架，利用大语言模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro）、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类构建认知孪生。通过在40名参与者为期七周的研究中进行自动评估（F1约75%）、用户评估（Likert评分约4.3）和语义对齐验证（主题一致性从0.436提升至0.626），证明PTM输出具有可接受的保真度，能反映用户思维模式，并呈现逐层语义抽象的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习者模型缺乏层次化、可解释的表征能力，难以真实克隆学习者的思维模式并构建认知孪生，亟需一种既能体现个性化认知结构又具备可解释性的层次化表示方法。

Method: 构建五层结构（行为实例→行为模式→认知常规→元认知倾向→自我系统价值），采用大语言模型推理（Gemini 2.5 Pro）、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类的流水线，并引入人在环中（HITL）精炼机制。

Result: 自动评估F1达74.57%（HITL前）和75.48%（HITL后）；用户Likert评分均值4.26和4.30；主题一致性从行为层的0.436提升至核心价值层的0.626，词汇重叠从0.114降至0.007。

Conclusion: PTM能以可接受的保真度生成分层学习者表征，用户普遍认为模型反映了其思维模式，且各层呈现了从具体到抽象的语义递进模式，验证了基于Marzano理论构建认知孪生的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents the Personalized Thinking Model (PTM), a hierarchical and interpretable learner representation designed for AI supported education. PTM organizes evidence from learner journals into a five-layer structure covering behavioral instances, behavioral patterns, cognitive routines, metacognitive tendencies, and self-system values. PTM is grounded in Marzano's New Taxonomy of Educational Objectives and tries to clone learner's thinking model and build cognitive twin. It was constructed using a pipeline that combines large language model inference (Gemini 2.5 Pro), sentence embeddings, dimensionality reduction, and consensus clustering. This paper evaluates PTM fidelity through three methods applied to 40 participants in a seven-week study. First, automatic evaluation using atomic information point matching yielded an overall F1 score of 74.57% before human-in-the-loop (HITL) refinement and 75.48% after refinement. Second, user evaluation using a Likert scale produced mean ratings of 4.26 and 4.30 on a five-point scale for pre and post-HITL conditions respectively. Third, semantic alignment verification showed that topic coherence increased from 0.436 at the behavioral layer to 0.626 at the core value layer, while lexical overlap with journal vocabulary decreased from 0.114 to 0.007 across those same layers. These results suggest that the PTM produces outputs with acceptable fidelity, was generally perceived by users as reflecting their thinking, and showed a pattern consistent with semantic abstraction across layers.

</details>


### [141] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了幂分布（power distribution）在采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习和自蒸馏之间的桥梁作用，证明了幂分布是KL正则化RL的闭式优化解，并提出了幂自蒸馏（power self-distillation）方法，在推理任务上能以更低推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对RL是否真正负责LLM强推理能力提出质疑，同时蒸馏和推理时采样（包括幂采样）被证明有效，但RL、蒸馏和采样之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在厘清三者间的理论联系。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明幂分布桥接了采样、自奖励KL正则化RL和自蒸馏；2）从采样角度：指出廉价的局部近似无法在不了解可能后缀的情况下复现序列级幂分布；3）从RL角度：证明幂分布是以模型序列级对数概率为奖励的KL正则化RL的闭式优化解；4）提出幂自蒸馏：将幂采样的成本摊销到教师样本的监督训练中；5）理论分析：幂自蒸馏可实现自奖励锐化，下游真奖励的提升由真奖励与自奖励在幂分布下的协方差决定。

Result: 推理任务实验支持理论分析：幂采样提高了自奖励；真奖励的增益取决于与自奖励的对齐程度；幂自蒸馏能以更低的推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。

Conclusion: 幂分布是连接采样、KL正则化RL和自蒸馏的统一框架；幂自蒸馏是高效的离线蒸馏替代方案，兼顾性能与推理成本。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [142] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的方法，用于从环境时间序列中预测葡萄园病害风险，将传统每日分类任务重构为预测未来3-7天内的病害风险期转换，并评估了XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将病害预测建模为每日存在性分类，容易偏向持续性预测，且无法为可操作的短时预警提供足够支持。

Method: 将任务重构为预测未来3-7天窗口内标注的病害风险期转换；定义最小无病害间隔以减少标签碎片化；构建包含湿度动态、降雨累积、温度变异和季节性循环编码的输入表示；评估XGBoost、LSTM和TCN。

Result: 基于事件的方法支持实用的短时预警；不同模型在事件召回率、提前时间和误报行为之间表现出不同的权衡。

Conclusion: 问题公式化在环境时间序列学习中至关重要，基于事件的预测方法对葡萄园病害预警系统具有实际价值。

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [143] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EP-GRPO框架，通过熵-进度对齐机制解决GRPO在token级信用分配中的三类失败问题（非均匀信息价值、极性错配、零方差坍缩），显著提升数学推理任务的准确率和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在强化学习奖励验证中存在三类信用分配失败：均匀token粒度忽略信息异质性、均匀极性导致错误奖励与正确惩罚、零方差坍缩消除结果驱动梯度。需系统性量化并解决这些问题。

Method: 提出EP-GRPO框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 熵门控调制（优先处理高熵决策关键点）；2) 隐式过程信号（利用策略散度结合结果优势产生token级方向性反馈，无需外部奖励模型）；3) 累积熵映射（实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下自然维持梯度流）。

Result: 在数学推理基准实验上，EP-GRPO相比GRPO及其变体取得了更优的准确率和训练效率。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过挖掘模型内在信息流提供密集的自监督引导，有效解决了GRPO的信用分配问题，提升了推理能力与训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
  To address these limitations, we propose Entropy-Progress Aligned GRPO (EP-GRPO), a framework that mines the model's intrinsic information flow for dense, self-supervised guidance. EP-GRPO integrates entropy-gated modulation to prioritize high entropy decision pivots, implicit process signals from policy divergence anchored to outcome advantages for directional token-level feedback without external reward models, and cumulative entropy mapping that enables progress-aligned advantage normalization, naturally maintaining gradient flow under zero reward variance.
  Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EP-GRPO achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to GRPO and its variants. The code will be available.

</details>


### [144] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAAST提出了一种前向传播的联想适配方法，通过单次分析编译将标注示例转化为快速权重，消除了对记忆或上下文依赖，实现了常量时间推理，在图像分类和语言建模任务上匹配或超越反向传播适配，同时减少90%以上适配时间和95%内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练模型适配面临两难：反向传播训练成本高，而基于记忆或上下文学习推理开销大。作者希望找到一种既高效又低资源消耗的适配方法。

Method: 提出FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation），一种纯前向的联想适配方法，通过单次分析编译将标注样本解析为快速权重（fast weights），无需反向传播，也不依赖外部记忆或上下文。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准上，FAAST匹配或超越基于反向传播的适配方法，适配时间减少90%以上；与基于记忆/上下文的适配方法相比，内存节省高达95%。实现常量时间推理，任务适配与预训练表征解耦。

Conclusion: FAAST是一种高效、可扩展的监督任务适配解决方案，尤其适合资源受限的模型，在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [145] [Reliable Modeling of Distribution Shifts via Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04965)
*Philip Naumann,Jacob Kauffmann,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位移重塑最优传输（ReshapeOT），通过将样本位移的二阶矩估计为马氏距离来重塑基础度量，从而在输入空间中构建"高速通道"，使传输方案更符合观测到的位移模式。该方法计算轻量，可集成到任何基于代价矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化扩展。实验表明其在传输可靠性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输在直接比较输入空间中的分布时，基础度量的设计至关重要，不当的度量会导致优化结果违背真实的位移几何结构。现有方法缺乏利用观测样本位移作为额外知识来引导传输的能力，因此需要一种能整合位移信息来重塑度量的方法。

Method: 将欧几里得度量替换为从位移二阶矩估计得到的马氏距离，通过在输入空间中构建"高速通道"来引导传输方案，使其与观测位移对齐。该方法计算轻量，可无缝集成到任何基于代价矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化扩展。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性上取得了显著提升，并在两个实际应用场景中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将观测位移作为额外知识融入基础度量重塑，ReshapeOT有效提升了最优传输的可靠性，且方法轻量灵活，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) is a central framework for modeling distribution shifts. Because OT compares distributions directly in input space, a well-designed ground metric between observations is essential to ensure that the optimizer does not violate the true geometry of change. We propose Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT), a method that reshapes the ground metric by integrating observed sample displacements as an additional source of knowledge. Technically, ReshapeOT replaces the Euclidean metric with a Mahalanobis distance estimated from displacement second moments. This effectively carves expressways through the input space, inviting transport solutions that better align with observed displacements. Our method is computationally lightweight, integrates seamlessly into any OT solver that operates on a cost matrix, and can be kernelized for further flexibility. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that ReshapeOT achieves substantial gains in transport reliability. We further demonstrate our method's usefulness in two practical use cases.

</details>


### [146] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种阈值引导的对齐框架（Threshold-Guided Alignment），通过将成对偏好优化转化为基于标量评分的二分类任务，并引入置信加权项，实现了无需成对比较即可对齐视觉生成模型的方法，在扩散和掩码生成范式上均优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对偏好优化方法依赖标注好的偏好对，但实际中反馈常以独立标量评分形式收集，这限制了方法的可扩展性。作者希望利用更易获取的标量反馈来对齐视觉生成模型。

Method: 重新审视KL正则化对齐目标，证明最优策略隐式地将每个样本的奖励与一个实例相关的基线进行比较。提出用数据驱动的全局阈值（基于经验评分统计估计）替代该不可解基线，将对齐转化为对未配对数据的二分类任务，并引入置信加权项以强调偏离阈值较大的样本。

Result: 在扩散和掩码生成两种范式上，跨越三个测试集和五个奖励模型，该方法在偏好对齐指标上一致优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 阈值引导框架是一种简单而有原则的替代方案，无需成对比较即可有效对齐视觉生成模型，具有良好的可扩展性和样本效率。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [147] [Skill Neologisms: Towards Skill-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04970)
*Antonin Berthon,Nicolas Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出技能新词（skill neologisms）——即在模型词汇表中集成并优化特定技能的软token——作为无需权重更新即可选择性扩展LLM能力的方法。实验表明，预训练LLM已存在与程序性知识相关的token，且技能新词可学习以提升特定技能表现，同时能与分布外技能组合，独立训练的技能新词可实现零样本组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展模型能力的方法存在缺陷：微调及参数高效变体存在灾难性遗忘风险，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受限于有效上下文长度。需要一种可扩展、无遗忘且可组合的能力扩展方式。

Method: 提出技能新词概念，即在模型词汇表中添加软token，通过优化这些token来提升特定技能的表现，无需更新模型权重。首先观察预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token，然后学习技能新词并测试其可组合性。

Result: 1）预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token；2）技能新词可学习并提升特定技能表现；3）技能新词可与分布外技能组合；4）独立训练的技能新词可实现零样本组合。

Conclusion: 技能新词为基于技能的持续学习提供了一条可扩展的路径，能够在避免灾难性遗忘的同时实现能力的灵活扩展与组合。

Abstract: Modern LLMs show mastery over an ever-growing range of skills, as well as the ability to compose them flexibly.
