### [184] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究随机多臂老虎机和情节强化学习中的遗憾分布，提出一种带有特殊探索奖励的UCBVI-style算法，推导出通用的分布遗憾界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂老虎机分布遗憾界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注遗憾的期望上界，对其分布特性（如尾部风险）理解不足。本文旨在建立统一的分布遗憾界理论框架，刻画遗憾在不同置信水平下的完整分布特征。

Method: 提出UCBVI-style算法，采用形如min{c₁ₖ/N, c₂ₖ/√N}的探索奖励。对于任意用户指定的参数序列(c₁ₖ, c₂ₖ)，推导出通用的gap-independent和gap-dependent分布遗憾界。

Result: 在minimax和instance-dependent两种情境下均实现了期望遗憾与分布遗憾之间的最优权衡。对于A臂T步的多臂老虎机，得到O(√(AT)log(1/δ))的分布遗憾界。

Conclusion: 首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂老虎机分布遗憾界的猜想，为随机多臂老虎机和情节强化学习提供了统一的分布遗憾分析框架。

Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

</details>


### [185] [Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05112)
*Tianshu Zhu,Wenyu Zhang,Xiaoying Zuo,Lun Tian,Haotian Zhao,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prefix Sampling (PS)方法，通过重放轨迹前缀将采样组的通过率控制在50%这个信息量最大的操作点，从而在SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习中实现2.01倍(14B)和1.55倍(32B)的端到端加速，同时提升或保持最终验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench风格的智能体强化学习依赖昂贵的带状态轨迹，但大量计算浪费在通过率偏斜的采样组上，二值奖励提供的对比信号较弱。作者将这种低效性归结为通过率控制问题，并论证50%通过率是最具信息量的操作点。

Method: 提出Prefix Sampling (PS)：重放轨迹前缀来引导偏斜组向50%通过率靠拢——成功前缀作为失败组的"先发优势"，失败前缀作为成功组的"惩罚负担"。在有状态智能体环境中，通过重放重建前缀状态，同时将重放的token从损失中排除，仅优化当前策略生成的延续部分。

Result: 在SWE-bench风格智能体RL上，Qwen3-14B获得2.01倍加速，Qwen3-32B获得1.55倍加速；14B的SWE-bench Verified峰值从0.273提升至0.295。在AIME 2025数学推理实验中也验证了相同的通过率控制模式。

Conclusion: 通过率控制是提升智能体RL样本效率的有效原则，Prefix Sampling通过重放机制在保持或提升性能的同时显著加速训练，且增益可分解为重放、双向覆盖和自适应控制三个部分。

Abstract: SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive structure. Guided by this principle, we propose Prefix Sampling (PS), which replays trajectory prefixes to steer skewed groups toward this regime: successful prefixes serve as head starts for mostly failing groups, while failing prefixes serve as handicaps for mostly passing groups. In stateful agent environments, prefix states are reconstructed through replay while replayed tokens are excluded from the loss, restricting optimization to continuations generated by the current policy. On SWE-bench-style agentic RL, PS delivers end-to-end wall-clock speedups of 2.01x on Qwen3-14B and 1.55x on Qwen3-32B while preserving or improving final verified performance. For 14B, the SWE-bench Verified peak rises from the baseline peak of 0.273 to 0.295 under PS. Additional mathematical reasoning experiments on AIME 2025 show the same pass-rate control pattern and decompose the gains into replay, bidirectional coverage, and adaptive control.

</details>


### [186] [How Long Does Infinite Width Last? Signal Propagation in Long-Range Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05113)
*Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性循环模型在有限宽度下的信号传播问题，推导了隐状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式，识别出三个联合深度-宽度缩放机制：亚临界（t=o(√n)）、临界（t~c√n）和超临界（t≫√n），揭示了无限宽理论在长程线性循环中失效的精确深度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信号传播理论主要依赖无限宽极限假设，但当循环深度t与宽度n共同增长时，该近似何时失效尚不明确。这一问题对于现代循环序列模型尤为重要，因其自然工作状态涉及长输入序列（即大t）。

Method: 推导了复高斯初始化下线性循环中隐状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式，并基于这些公式识别出联合深度-宽度缩放机制。

Result: 发现了三个缩放机制：(i) 亚临界t=o(√n)：无限宽近似有效；(ii) 临界t~c√n：出现不可忽略的偏差，非平凡联合缩放极限；(iii) 超临界t≫√n：有限宽效应主导。同时发现循环模型中有限宽效应随深度累积比前馈模型更快。

Conclusion: 精确定位了无限宽理论在长程线性循环中失效的深度尺度，表明标准初始化方案（如Glorot）在超临界时变得不稳定，揭示了循环模型与前馈模型在信号传播行为上的本质差异。

Abstract: We study signal propagation in linear recurrent models at finite width. While existing signal propagation theory relies predominantly on the infinite-width limit, it remains unclear for how long that approximation remains accurate when recurrent depth $t$ grows jointly with width $n$. This question is especially relevant for modern recurrent sequence models, whose natural operating regime involves long input sequences, i.e., large $t$. We derive exact finite-width formulas for the hidden state signal energies in linear recurrences under complex Gaussian initialization. Using these formulas, we identify the joint depth-width scaling regimes that govern signal propagation: (i) a subcritical regime $t=o(\sqrt n)$, in which the infinite-width approximation remains valid; (ii) a critical regime $t\sim c\sqrt n$, in which non-negligible deviations from infinite-width predictions appear and a nontrivial joint scaling limit emerges; and (iii) a supercritical regime $t\gg \sqrt n$, in which finite-width effects dominate. Thus, our results pinpoint the precise recurrent depth scale at which infinite-width theory breaks down in long-range linear recurrences. In turn, this shows when standard initialization schemes, such as Glorot, become unstable. More broadly, our results demonstrate that finite-width effects accumulate more rapidly with depth in recurrent models than in feedforward ones, leading to qualitatively different signal propagation behavior.

</details>


### [187] [Manifold Steering Reveals the Shared Geometry of Neural Network Representation and Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05115)
*Daniel Wurgaft,Can Rager,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Sheridan Feucht,Usha Bhalla,Tal Haklay,Eric Bigelow,Raphael Sarfati,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Noah Goodman,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过干预激活空间中的路径，验证了神经表征的几何结构对行为存在因果影响。研究发现，沿表征流形（而非线性方向）进行干预，能使模型产生更自然的行为轨迹，且优化干预方向恢复的表征路径会自然弯曲，证实了表征几何与行为几何之间存在双向因果关系。作者在不同任务和模态（语言模型推理、上下文学习、视频世界模型）中验证了这一发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征具有丰富的几何结构，但这种结构是否因果性地塑造了行为尚不清楚。作者希望检验表征几何与行为几何之间的因果联系。

Method: 先分别拟合激活流形M_h和行为流形M_y，然后通过干预进行双向验证：(1) 沿流形M_h干预（流形干预）能否产生沿M_y的行为轨迹；(2) 优化干预以产生沿M_y的路径能否恢复出沿M_h弯曲的激活轨迹。

Result: 流形干预产生沿行为流形的自然轨迹，而线性干预则偏离流形产生不自然输出；优化行为轨迹可恢复出弯曲的激活流形路径。

Conclusion: 神经表征的几何结构并非偶然，而是实现原则性内部干预控制的正确对象。核心问题从"找到正确的方向"转变为"找到正确的几何"。

Abstract: Neural representations carry rich geometric structure; but does that structure causally shape behavior? To address this question, we intervene along paths through activation space defined by different geometries, and measure the behavioral trajectories they induce. In particular, we test whether interventions that respect the geometry of activation space will yield behaviors close to those the model exhibits naturally. Concretely, we first fit an activation manifold $M_h$ to representations and a behavior manifold $M_y$ to output probability distributions. We then test the link $M_h \leftrightarrow M_y$ via interventions: we find that steering along $M_h$, which we term manifold steering, yields behavioral trajectories that follow $M_y$, while linear steering -- which assumes a Euclidean geometry -- cuts through off-manifold regions and hence produces unnatural outputs. Moreover, optimizing interventions in activation space to produce paths along $M_y$ recovers activation trajectories that trace the curvature of $M_h$. We demonstrate this bidirectional relationship between the geometry of representation and behavior across tasks and modalities. In language models, we use reasoning tasks with cyclic and sequential geometries as well as in-context learning tasks with more complex graph geometries. In a video world model, we use a task with geometry corresponding to physical dynamics. Overall, our work shows that geometry in neural representation is not merely incidental, but is in fact the proper object for enabling principled control via intervention on internals. This recasts the core problem of steering from finding the right direction to finding the right geometry.

</details>


### [188] [On the Hardness of Junking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05116)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了"自然后门"（junking问题）的发现难度。形式化了该问题——寻找最大化有害响应目标前缀生成概率的token序列，并提出贪心随机搜索方法。结果表明该问题比标准越狱攻击更难，但简单策略仍能以高成功率解决，且发现的token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持其隐含地来源于训练过程的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有观察表明无需对抗性提示仅靠优化的token序列就能触发有害行为（即存在自然后门），但该设置仍未被充分探索，特别是发现自然后门的难度尚未被评估。

Method: 形式化junking问题为寻找最大化有害响应目标前缀生成概率的token序列；提出贪心随机搜索方法进行求解。

Result: Junking问题比标准越狱攻击更难；但简单的贪心随机搜索策略仍能以高成功率解决该问题；发现的token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域。

Conclusion: 语义信息在提示设计中至关重要；自然后门确实存在且易于恢复；发现的token序列处于低概率区域，支持其隐含地从训练过程中涌现的假设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which typically rely on carefully designed prompts containing explicit semantic structure. These attacks generally operate by fixing an adversarial instruction and optimizing small adversarial components (e.g., suffixes or prefixes). In this setting, prompt structure is fundamental for performance, and recent results show that even simple random search can achieve strong performance when combined with sophisticated prompt design. Recently, it has been observed that harmful behaviors can be elicited even without the adversarial prompt, relying solely on optimized token sequences. This suggests the existence of natural backdoors, i.e., token sequences naturally emerged during LLMs training that trigger unsafe outputs without any meaningful instruction. However, despite these observations, this setting remains largely unexplored, and in particular the hardness of finding natural backdoors has not been assessed yet. In this work, we provide a first proof-of-concept study investigating the hardness of this task, which we refer to as the junking problem. We formalize it as the problem of finding token sequences that maximize the probability of generating a target prefix of harmful responses, propose a greedy random-search method to assess is such sequences can be discovered easily. Our results show that this problem is harder than standard jailbreak attacks, confirming the importance of semantic information in prompt design. At the same time, we find that our simple strategy is sufficient to solve it with a high success rate, suggesting that natural backdoors are present and easily recoverable. Finally, through perplexity analysis, we observe that the discovered token sequences lie in low-probability regions of the model distribution, supporting the hypothesis that they emerged implicitly from the training process.

</details>


### [189] [Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05120)
*Sahar Askari,Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi,Fatemeh Ensafdoust,Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释且可扩展的框架，利用多模态生理信号（EEG、EMG、GSR）通过特征选择、贝叶斯超参数优化和加权软投票集成（XGBoost+LightGBM）解码驾驶行为，最终集成模型在测试集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，SHAP分析验证了多模态融合的必要性和生理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶行为解码方法通常依赖单一模态信号，忽视了多模态生理信号互补信息的价值，且缺乏可解释性和可扩展性。本研究旨在通过融合EEG、EMG和GSR三种模态，构建一个既高效又具有生理可解释性的驾驶行为解码框架。

Method: 研究首先对EEG、EMG和GSR多模态生理信号进行严格的预处理，然后设计面向领域的特征提取流程，涵盖时域、频域及衍生的生理指标。采用基于SHAP的精英特征选择策略保留250个最优特征以降低维度。使用Optuna实现贝叶斯优化对XGBoost和LightGBM进行超参数调优，最终构建加权软投票集成模型融合两者优势。

Result: 集成模型在测试集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线模型和传统机器学习模型。消融实验表明，多模态融合相比最佳单模态（EEG）提升了8%的性能。SHAP分析显示EEG贡献了大部分预测权重，GSR和EMG特征对高唤醒度和运动密集型驾驶操作提供了关键的判别信号。

Conclusion: 多模态生理信号融合结合梯度提升集成方法可有效解码驾驶行为，SHAP特征选择和分析在维持模型可解释性的同时降低了计算开销，验证了各模态的生理学合理性和互补价值。

Abstract: An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.

</details>


### [190] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的方法，利用电子健康记录（EHR）中既往患者病例检测异常的患者管理行为，并通过4,486名心脏术后患者的数据和专家评估验证了该方法在低误报率下识别潜在错误的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假设患者管理行为中与既往病例显著偏离的情况可能源于潜在错误，值得触发警报，从而提升患者安全。

Method: 基于EHR中存储的既往患者病例，构建数据驱动的异常检测模型，通过比较当前患者管理行为与历史模式的偏离程度来识别异常；使用4,486名心脏术后患者数据进行评估，并以专家意见作为金标准。

Result: 异常检测警报的误报率较低，且异常强度与警报率呈正相关。

Conclusion: 基于异常的警报方法在临床环境中具有可行性，能够以合理的低误报率识别潜在错误，且更强的异常信号对应更高的警报率，支持其在患者安全监测中的应用价值。

Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

</details>


### [191] [Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05134)
*Dan Wilson,Mohamed Akrout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman算子理论的单次采样幻觉检测方法，将LLM视为黑箱动力系统，通过嵌入向量序列的动力学建模实现高效幻觉检测，无需二次采样或外部知识检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法依赖计算昂贵的采样一致性检查或外部知识检索，效率低、资源开销大，亟需一种低成本、单次推理即可完成的检测方案。

Method: 1) 将LLM响应通过嵌入模型投影到高维流形，得到向量序列作为潜状态空间动力学的观测；2) 利用Koopman算子理论分别拟合"事实性"和"幻觉性"两种状态下的转移算子；3) 基于两种转移算子的预测误差定义差分残差分数；4) 引入偏好感知校准机制，通过少量示范样本优化分类阈值。

Result: 在三个数据基准上的实验表明，该方法在资源开销更低的条件下达到最先进的检测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过单次采样即可实现高效幻觉检测，避免了二次采样或外部知识检索，为低资源场景下的幻觉检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.

</details>


### [192] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了一项由高中生和低年级本科生在研究生导师指导下，借助AI工具进行金融预测的科研项目。项目强调工作流设计而非传统教学，学生通过AI迭代编写代码，并在日常站会中解决概念问题。尽管学生背景有限，但成功构建了有实际意义的ETF价格预测模型，验证了AI工具在加速学习、聚焦高阶问题方面的教育价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在AI工具辅助下，如何让技术背景有限的高中生直接参与高阶问题定义与解决，而非传统先学方法再应用的顺序教学模式。

Method: 采用工作流设计教学法：学生自主识别解决问题所需的步骤序列，然后使用AI驱动工具执行每个步骤；通过日常站会进行代码调试和概念答疑；学生基于个人兴趣深入计算机科学或金融领域。

Result: 学生在有限的技术背景下，通过AI工具构建了有实际应用意义的技术市场分析及ETF价格预测模型；每位学生在各自兴趣领域实现了深入探索。

Conclusion: AI工具能够作为指导高中生的有效教学手段，使学生聚焦于问题定义和高阶概念理解，显著加速学习进程，是重要的教育学实践探索。

Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [From Language to Logic: A Theoretical Architecture for VLM-Grounded Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04327)
*Kristy Sakano,Kalonji Harrington,Mumu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将人类安全规则和操作员偏好整合到自主机器人户外导航的架构，通过自然语言转STL规范、VLM零样本场景理解以及形式化满意度度量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化户外环境中，自主机器人导航需要同时满足高层安全规则和操作员的语义偏好，而现有方法难以将自然语言指令与形式化导航约束有效结合。

Method: 1) 将自然语言规则翻译为信号时序逻辑(STL)规范；2) 持久性环境和地形偏好映射到2D代价地图；3) 时态动态需求通过运行时监控的STL规范表达；4) 使用VLM进行零样本场景理解，建立人类指令、语义特征和环境约束之间的映射；5) 通过嵌入环境属性和运行时监控的形式化满意度度量构建导航模型。

Result: 构建了一个示例性导航模型，能够满足STL编码的规范和操作员偏好。

Conclusion: 该架构通过形式化方法将自然语言安全规则和偏好整合进导航系统，利用VLM实现零样本语义理解，为户外非结构化环境中的自主导航提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for integrating high-level, human-provided safety rules and operator-aligned semantic preferences into autonomous robot navigation in unstructured outdoor environments. In our approach, natural-language rules are translated into Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that guide planning and navigation during runtime. Persistent, environment-centric rules and terrain preferences are grounded into a 2D cost map, while temporally dynamic requirements are expressed as STL specifications to be monitored during runtime. We hypothesize the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for zero-shot scene understanding, enabling mapping between human instructions, semantic features, and environmental constraints. Within this framework, we construct an illustrative navigation model that is designed to satisfy a set of STL-encoded specifications and soft operator preferences through formal satisfaction metrics embedded into environmental properties and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [194] [Conditional Flow-VAE for Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04366)
*Zimu Gong,Brian Zhaoning Zhang,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件潜流匹配的方法，用于可扩展且真实地生成自动驾驶安全关键场景，通过分布匹配将常规场景转换为安全关键轨迹，并融合仿真与真实数据，实验表明该方法能更一致、真实地生成多样化安全关键场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景对自动驾驶开发至关重要，但真实驾驶数据中极为罕见；手动设计测试用例缺乏可扩展性，对抗优化又常产生不真实行为，因此需要一种可扩展且真实的安全关键场景生成方法。

Method: 提出条件潜流匹配（conditional latent flow matching）方法，利用分布匹配将常规（nominal）场景转换为安全关键（safety-critical）轨迹；同时整合仿真数据和真实世界数据，实现多样化、数据驱动的场景生成。

Result: 该方法能更一致且更真实地生成新颖的安全关键场景，相比现有方法在生成质量和多样性上表现更优。

Conclusion: 提出的方法可作为训练和基准测试自动驾驶系统的有效工具，兼顾场景生成的可扩展性与真实性。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are essential for the development of autonomous vehicles (AVs) but are rare in real-world driving data. While simulation offers a way to generate such scenarios, manually designed test cases lack scalability, and adversarial optimization often produces unrealistic behaviors. In this work, we introduce a conditional latent flow matching approach for scalable and realistic safety-critical scenario generation. Our method uses distribution matching to transform nominal scenes into safety-critical rollouts. Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating both simulation and real-world data enables our framework to efficiently generate diverse, data-driven scenarios. Experimental results highlight that our approach is able to more consistently and realistically generate novel safety-critical scenarios, making it a valuable tool for training and benchmarking AV systems.

</details>


### [195] [Autonomous Laparoscope Control through Unified Mechanics-Based Representation of Multimodal Intraoperative Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04408)
*Xiaojian Li,Jin Fang,Yudong Shi,Xilin Xiao,Kai Yan,Kang Min,Ling Li,Hua Tang,Hangjie Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于统一力学建模的腹腔镜持镜机器人控制方法，将位置、力/力矩和图像等多模态信息统一映射为等效 wrench 表示，通过任务优先级投影合成控制命令，在保持 RCM 约束的同时实现 compliant 拖动和自主器械跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中多模态信号（位置、力/力矩、图像）物理意义和单位差异显著，难以建立统一表示并生成可直接用于腹腔镜控制的命令。

Method: 1) 设计多种术中信号（位置、力/力矩、图像）的映射策略，统一为操作空间中的等效 wrench 表示；2) 采用任务优先级方案，将 wrench 分别注入任务空间和零空间，通过任务优先级投影合成控制命令；3) 以 RCM 位置、力传感器读数、腹腔镜图像为例，构建 RCM 约束 wrench、腹腔镜操控 wrench 和器械跟踪 wrench。

Result: 在手术体模和体内猪试验中验证了该方法支持多任务操作，包括 compliant 腹腔镜操控和自主器械跟踪，同时维持 RCM 约束并降低 trocar 部位持续载荷。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了多模态信息的统一表示与协调融合，在一个框架内同时支持多任务操作。

Abstract: Laparoscope-holding robots can provide surgeons with a stable laparoscopic field of view (FOV) and reduce the burden on human assistants. To maintain an ideal intraoperative FOV, the robot must continuously adjust the laparoscope pose according to intraoperative information. However, intraoperative multimodal signals, such as position, force/torque, and images, differ markedly in physical meaning and units, making it difficult to build a unified representation and to generate control commands that can be used directly for laparoscope control. To address this issue, we propose a laparoscope-holding robot control method based on unified mechanics modeling of multimodal information. First, we design mapping strategies for multiple intraoperative sources, including position, force/torque, and images, and unify them into an equivalent-wrench representation in the operational space. Then, using a task-priority scheme, we inject the wrenches into the task space and the null space, respectively, and synthesize laparoscope control commands via task-priority projection, thereby achieving consistent representation and coordinated fusion of multimodal information within a single framework. Finally, taking the intraoperative remote center of motion (RCM) position, force/torque sensor readings, and laparoscopic images as examples, we construct an RCM-constraint wrench to enforce the RCM geometric constraint and reduce the contact force at the trocar site, a laparoscope-manipulation wrench to enable compliant dragging, and an instrument-tracking wrench to achieve autonomous visual tracking of the instruments. Experiments on a surgical phantom and in vivo porcine trials demonstrate that the proposed method supports multi-task operation, including compliant laparoscope manipulation and autonomous instrument tracking, while maintaining the RCM constraint and reducing sustained trocar-site loading.

</details>


### [196] [Tightly-Coupled Estimation and Guidance for Robust Low-Thrust Rendezvous via Adaptive Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04481)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧密耦合的估计-制导架构，通过导航置信度在线调节间接最优控制求解器的同伦参数，在测量严重退化条件下将终端脱靶量从数百米降至亚米级，同时保持计算实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小燃料低推力交会制导产生的bang-bang控制结构对估计误差、传感器异常和求解器正则化高度敏感，导致非合作近距离操作中闭环执行脆弱，现有方法在测量降级时表现不佳。

Method: 在Clohessy-Wiltshire坐标系中建模相对运动，采用线性Kalman滤波估计平动状态并引入多调谐因子(MTF)协方差膨胀机制抑制异常新息方向；将归一化新息与MTF活动度组合为复合分数，在线映射到滚动时域间接最优控制求解器的同伦参数，使控制器在置信度下降时平滑过渡到保守模式，测量改善时恢复bang-bang控制。

Result: 固定bang-bang制导在严重测量退化下脆性明显；普通KF和MTF-KF固定同伦参数控制器均产生数百米终端脱靶量；所提MTF自适应同伦控制器将终端脱靶量降低约两个数量级（从百米级降至亚米级），控制代价仅适度增加。自适应同伦是主导鲁棒性机制，MTF提供额外精度和效率改进。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了快速可靠的求解时间，支持实际在线部署可行性，为低推力交会制导在非合作场景下的鲁棒闭环执行提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Minimum-fuel low-thrust rendezvous guidance yields bang-bang control structures highly sensitive to estimation errors, sensor anomalies, and solver regularization, making aggressive closed-loop execution brittle for uncooperative proximity operations. This paper proposes a tightly-coupled estimation and guidance architecture where navigation confidence directly modulates the homotopy parameter of a receding-horizon indirect optimal control solver. Relative motion is modeled in the Clohessy-Wiltshire frame. The translational state is estimated via a linear Kalman filter augmented by a Multiple Tuning Factors (MTF) covariance inflation mechanism that suppresses suspicious innovation directions. A composite score from the normalized innovation and MTF activity is mapped online to the homotopy parameter, allowing the controller to relax toward a smoother, conservative regime when confidence degrades, and recover fuel-efficient bang-bang control as sensing improves. Numerical results under severe measurement degradation show fixed bang-bang guidance remains brittle; both plain-KF and MTF-KF fixed-epsilon controllers yield large terminal miss distances. Conversely, the proposed MTF-adaptive homotopy controller reduces terminal miss by roughly two orders of magnitude, from hundreds of meters to sub-meter levels, requiring only a moderate increase in control effort versus the open-loop fuel-optimal benchmark. A comparison indicates adaptive homotopy is the dominant robustness mechanism, while MTF provides additional accuracy and efficiency improvements. The receding-horizon implementation exhibits consistently fast and reliable solution times, supporting the practical online viability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [197] [HDFlow: Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow Planning for Long-horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04525)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,Chaoyi Xu,Weiheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化扩散流规划框架(HDFlow)，结合高层扩散规划器与低层整流流规划器，在长时域稀疏奖励任务中实现高效分层规划，在家具组装及多类长期任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式规划方法在长时域稀疏奖励任务中缺乏原则性的层次分解框架，且迭代去噪过程导致实时执行计算开销大。

Method: HDFlow采用高层扩散规划器在隐空间生成策略性子目标序列，利用扩散的强探索能力；低层整流流规划器则基于ODE生成平滑密集轨迹，兼顾速度与效率。

Result: 在四项家具组装任务(仿真与真实)及两类长期基准任务( locomotion与 manipulation)中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HDFlow通过层次化结合扩散与整流流模型，有效解决了单一范式生成式规划器的局限性，实现了高效、可泛化的长时域规划。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have shown promise in generating behavior plans for long-horizon, sparse reward tasks. While these approaches have achieved promising results, they often lack a principled framework for hierarchical decomposition and struggle with the computational demands of real-time execution, due to their iterative denoising process. In this work, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow (HDFlow), a novel hierarchical planning framework that optimally leverages the strengths of diffusion and rectified flow models to overcome the limitations of single-paradigm generative planners. HDFlow employs a high-level diffusion planner to generate sequences of strategic subgoals in a learned latent space, capitalizing on diffusion's powerful exploratory capabilities. These subgoals then guide a low-level rectified flow planner that generates smooth and dense trajectories, exploiting the speed and efficiency of ordinary differential equation (ODE)-based trajectory generation. We evaluate HDFlow on four challenging furniture assembly tasks in both simulation and real-world, where it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, we also showcase our method's generalizability on two long-horizon benchmarks comprising diverse locomotion and manipulation tasks. Project website: https://hdflow-page.github.io/

</details>


### [198] [Practical validation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04564)
*Jian Wu,Ulrich Sander,Carol Flannagan,Jonas Bärgman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯ROPE框架的合成预碰撞场景等价性评估方法，通过引入分箱统计量来评估合成数据与真实数据在实际安全影响评估中的等效性，并以AEB系统的后碰撞场景案例验证了框架的有效性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成预碰撞场景的代表性对于通过虚拟仿真评估驾驶自动化系统的安全性至关重要，但目前缺乏稳健的方法来评估合成场景与现实场景之间的实际等价性。传统的显著性检验只能检测差异，不能建立等价性，存在方法论空白。

Method: 在先前贝叶斯ROPE等价性检验框架的基础上，引入基于分箱的方法来定义适当的统计量和等价性准则。提出了两种基于分箱的统计量来测量数据集之间在实际安全影响评估中有意义的分布差异。

Result: 通过AEB系统后碰撞场景的案例研究，框架提供了对实际等价性的定量评估，以及对数据集差异的诊断性见解。两种合成后碰撞场景数据集与参考数据集之间的等价性得到了测试和评估。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解释且原则性的实际等价性评估基础，虽以追尾场景为演示，但具有通用性和可扩展性，适用于多种合成数据应用场景中更广泛的验证需求。

Abstract: The representativeness of synthetic pre-crash scenarios is crucial for assessing the safety impact of Driving Automation Systems through virtual simulations. However, a gap remains in the robust evaluation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios' practical equivalence to their real-world counterparts; that is, whether they are similar enough for the intended assessment purpose. Conventional significance testing is inadequate, as it focuses on detecting differences rather than establishing practical equivalence. This study addresses the research gap by extending our previous work on a Bayesian Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE)-based equivalence testing framework by introducing a binning-based approach to define appropriate statistics and equivalence criteria. Two binning-based statistics are proposed to measure practically meaningful distributional differences between datasets in the context of safety impact assessment. The framework's applicability is demonstrated through a case study, which tests the practical equivalence of two synthetic rear-end pre-crash datasets with a previously developed reference dataset in the context of the safety impact assessment of an Automatic Emergency Braking system. The results show that the framework provides informative quantitative assessments of practical equivalence as well as diagnostic insights into the divergence of datasets. Although the demonstration focuses on rear-end pre-crash scenarios, the framework is generic and extensible to broader validation contexts, providing an interpretable and principled basis for practical equivalence assessment across diverse synthetic data applications.

</details>


### [199] [Right Model, Right Time: Real-Time Cascaded-Fidelity MPC for Bipedal Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04607)
*Franek Stark,Felix Wiebe,Shubham Vyas,Dennis Mronga,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多阶段全身模型预测控制方法，在近端采用详细全身模型，远端采用简化单刚体模型，通过SQP求解非线性最优控制问题，实现双足机器人稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双足步行控制中，全身模型预测控制计算复杂度过高，难以实时应用。本文旨在通过多阶段建模策略在保证预测能力的同时降低计算负担。

Method: 采用多阶段MPC框架：近端预测步使用详细全身模型，远端预测步使用简化的单刚体模型。使用序列二次规划（SQP）在acados中求解非线性最优控制问题。控制器基于预定义的接触时序和目标步行速度，无需预先选择落脚点即可优化关节力矩。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中，对18自由度双足机器人HyPer-2进行了验证，证明了该方法能够实现稳定的双足步行控制。

Conclusion: 多阶段全身模型预测控制方法有效降低了计算复杂度，同时保留了足够的预测能力，无需预设落脚点即可实现双足机器人的稳定行走控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-phase whole-body model predictive control approach for bipedal walking, combining a detailed whole-body model in the near horizon with a simplified single-rigid-body model in the later prediction steps. This reduces computational complexity while retaining prediction capabilities. The resulting nonlinear optimal control problem is solved using sequential quadratic programming (SQP) in acados. Using a prior specified contact schedule and a target walking speed, the controller optimizes joint torques without depending on prior selected foot step locations. The controller is validated in MuJoCo simulation on the 18-DoF bipedal robot HyPer-2

</details>


### [200] [Active Contact Sensing for Robust Robot-to-Human Object Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04610)
*Linfeng Li,Lin Shao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动感知方法，通过信息收集运动感知人手施加的力，利用贝叶斯线性模型区分牢固抓握与意外接触，在12名参与者、30种物体的实验中实现了97.5%的成功率，比基线方法高出30%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动感知方法难以区分机器人向人递物时手部是牢固抓握还是意外接触，导致泛化能力差，无法实现鲁棒交接。

Method: 机器人主动施加信息收集运动，感知人手施加的力，用贝叶斯线性模型对从机器人运动到人施加力的分段线性映射进行分布建模，从而检测牢固抓握并进行主动信息收集。

Result: 在12名参与者、30种不同刚性物体的实验中，该方法达到了97.5%的成功率，比两个常见基线方法高出30%以上。

Conclusion: 主动感知方法通过施加微小扰动并感知力响应，能有效区分牢固抓握与意外接触，显著提升机器人递物交接的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robot-to-human object handover is an essential skill for robot assistants, from serving drinks at home to passing surgical tools in the operating room. We expect robots to perform handover robustly -- to release the object only after a firm human grasp while ignoring incidental touches. Existing passive-sensing methods struggle to generalize across diverse objects and human behaviors, as they lack informative perturbations to disambiguate different contact conditions, such as firm grasp versus incidental touch. We propose an active sensing approach for robust handovers: the robot applies information-gathering motions and senses the resulting human-applied forces to infer the contact state. A firm grasp produces forces in multiple directions, while an accidental touch does not. To capture this distinction, we model the contact state with a Bayesian linear model: a distribution over piecewise-linear mappings from robot motions to human-applied forces. This model enables firm grasp detection and active information gathering. In experiments with 12 participants and 30 diverse rigid objects, our method achieved a 97.5% success rate -- over 30% higher than two common baselines.

</details>


### [201] [ReflectDrive-2: Reinforcement-Learning-Aligned Self-Editing for Discrete Diffusion Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04647)
*Huimin Wang,Yue Wang,Bihao Cui,Pengxiang Li,Ben Lu,Mingqian Wang,Tong Wang,Chuan Tang,Teng Zhang,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReflectDrive-2 是一种掩码离散扩散规划器，通过并行掩码解码生成离散轨迹令牌，并采用 AutoEdit 进行原地轨迹修正，结合两阶段训练（监督学习+强化学习）和高效解码堆栈，在 NAVSIM 上实现了 91.0 PDMS（摄像头输入）和 94.8 PDMS（最佳6选1），延迟仅 31.8 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法缺乏有效的轨迹修正机制，且端到端生成与编辑难以协同优化，导致规划性能受限。

Method: 将轨迹表示为离散令牌，通过掩码离散扩散并行解码生成；AutoEdit 利用同一模型在离散令牌空间进行原地轨迹重写；两阶段训练：先监督恢复受扰动的专家轨迹，再用强化学习对整个"决策-草稿-修正"流程进行端到端优化；设计高效反射解码堆栈，包括共享前缀 KV 复用、交替步进解码和融合设备端去掩码。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，摄像头输入下 PDMS 达 91.0，最佳6选1下达 94.8，推理延迟 31.8 ms。强化学习使 AutoEdit 的 PDMS 增益从 0.3 提升至 1.9。

Conclusion: 全流程强化学习对"草稿-编辑"协同至关重要，离散令牌空间加原地编辑的设计在性能和效率上均有显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce ReflectDrive-2, a masked discrete diffusion planner with separate action expert for autonomous driving that represents plans as discrete trajectory tokens and generates them through parallel masked decoding. This discrete token space enables in-place trajectory revision: AutoEdit rewrites selected tokens using the same model, without requiring an auxiliary refinement network. To train this capability, we use a two-stage procedure. First, we construct structure-aware perturbations of expert trajectories along longitudinal progress and lateral heading directions and supervise the model to recover the original expert trajectory. We then fine-tune the full decision--draft--reflect rollout with reinforcement learning (RL), assigning terminal driving reward to the final post-edit trajectory and propagating policy-gradient credit through full-rollout transitions. Full-rollout RL proves crucial for coupling drafting and editing: under supervised training alone, inference-time AutoEdit improves PDMS by at most $0.3$, whereas RL increases its gain to $1.9$. We also co-design an efficient reflective decoding stack for the decision--draft--reflect pipeline, combining shared-prefix KV reuse, Alternating Step Decode, and fused on-device unmasking. On NAVSIM, ReflectDrive-2 achieves $91.0$ PDMS with camera-only input and $94.8$ PDMS in a best-of-6 oracle setting, while running at $31.8$ ms average latency on NVIDIA Thor.

</details>


### [202] [From Reach to Insert: Tactile-Augmented Precision Assembly under Sub-Millimeter Tolerances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04649)
*Xinpan Meng,Siyao Huang,JingPu Yang,Muyuan Ma,Zhenghua Ma,Lijun Han,Gao Yuan,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合模仿学习和强化学习的触觉增强两阶段方法，用于高精度装配中的紧公差插入任务。第一阶段IL学习带位置泛化的接近策略，第二阶段RL执行插入并实现接触失败恢复。通过触觉组采样和触觉评价器设计，在0.05mm间隙下实现67%成功率，最大交互力降低60%，扭矩降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度装配中的紧公差插入任务中，微小位姿误差会导致卡阻或过大接触力，传统的鲁棒安全插入策略难以获得。

Method: 提出触觉增强的两阶段方法：第一阶段使用模仿学习（IL）学习带位置泛化的接近策略；第二阶段使用强化学习（RL）执行插入并实现接触失败恢复。引入触觉组采样增加关键接触段的数据覆盖，设计触觉评价器更准确评估策略值。

Result: 在五种孔几何形状和三种间隙设置下进行系统实验。0.05mm最紧间隙下达到67%成功率，最大交互力降低60%，扭矩降低44%。

Conclusion: 该方法在多种几何形状和间隙设置下显著提升插入性能，同时保持低接触力，验证了高精度装配中有效性和安全性的平衡。

Abstract: High-precision assembly frequently involves tight-tolerance insertions, where even slight pose errors can cause jamming or excessive interaction forces, making robust and safe insertion policies difficult to obtain. This paper proposes a tactile-augmented two-stage method that combines Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) for precision insertion tasks. In the first stage, IL learns a reaching policy with position generalization that grasps the peg and brings it to the vicinity of the target region. In the second stage, RL executes the insertion and enables recovery from failures during contact-rich interactions. To better exploit tactile feedback, we introduce tactile group sampling to increase coverage of critical contact segments during training, and design a tactile critic to more accurately evaluate policy values, improving insertion performance while maintaining low contact forces. We conduct systematic experiments across five hole geometries and three clearance settings. Results show that our method substantially improves insertion performance across all settings; under the most challenging 0.05\,mm clearance, it achieves a 67\% success rate while keeping contact forces low, reducing the maximum interaction force by 60\% and torque by 44\%, thereby validating both effectiveness and safety for precision assembly.

</details>


### [203] [AI-Aided Advancements in Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04672)
*Guy Damari,Zeev Yampolsky,Nadav Cohen,Arup Kumar Sahoo,Jeryes Danial,Felipe O. Silva,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本章综述了AI辅助AUV定位技术的最新进展，重点介绍了将惯性导航系统与多普勒测速仪和相机相结合的先进传感器融合架构，以及AI驱动的学习方法在增强惯性航位推算和自适应融合算法方面的变革性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于电磁波快速衰减导致卫星无线电信号不可用，以及海洋环境的动态不可预测性，AUV导航面临巨大挑战，亟需高精度定位方案。

Method: 采用AI辅助的传感器融合架构，集成惯性导航系统、多普勒测速仪和相机；在传统基于模型的滤波方法之外，引入AI驱动的学习方法用于惯性航位推算和自适应融合算法。

Result: AI驱动的学习方法和自适应融合算法显著提升了自主水下任务的导航精度，为高精度导航提供了全面路线图。

Conclusion: AI辅助的传感器融合与学习方法为解决AUV在复杂水下环境中的高精度导航问题提供了有效途径，是未来自主水下任务的关键技术方向。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) have become indispensable for deep-sea exploration, spanning critical scientific research and commercial applications. The rapid attenuation of electromagnetic waves renders satellite radio signals unavailable, while the dynamic unpredictability of the marine environment presents formidable navigation challenges. This chapter explores recent advancements in AI-aided AUV positioning, specifically focusing on advanced sensor fusion architectures that integrate inertial navigation systems with Doppler velocity logs and cameras. Beyond traditional model-based filtering, we examine the transformative emergence of AI-driven learning approaches in enhancing inertial dead-reckoning tasks and adaptive fusion algorithms. By addressing these recent milestones, this chapter provides a comprehensive roadmap for achieving the high-precision navigation essential for autonomous underwater missions.

</details>


### [204] [From Pixels to Tokens: A Systematic Study of Latent Action Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04678)
*Yihan Lin,Haoyang Li,Yang Li,Haitao Shen,Yihan Zhao,Chao Shao,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了潜动作监督在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的两种范式：基于图像的潜动作（正则化轨迹）和基于动作的潜动作（统一目标空间），并在统一VLA基线框架下对比了四种集成策略，发现将离散潜动作令牌直接监督VLM效果最佳，且两类潜动作各有适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用潜动作监督VLA模型的方法零散且缺乏系统比较，需要结构化的研究来理清不同潜动作监督策略的优劣与适用场景。

Method: 从两个视角（基于图像的潜动作正则化轨迹、基于动作的潜动作统一目标空间）出发，在统一VLA基线框架下实例化并比较了四种代表性集成策略。

Result: 发现任务-公式对应关系：基于图像的潜动作有利于长程推理和场景级泛化，基于动作的潜动作擅长复杂运动协调；直接使用离散潜动作令牌监督VLM效果最佳。

Conclusion: 实验为混合数据中潜动作监督的优势提供了初步洞见，为VLA训练指明了有前景的方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Latent actions serve as an intermediate representation that enables consistent modeling of vision-language-action (VLA) models across heterogeneous datasets. However, approaches to supervising VLAs with latent actions are fragmented and lack a systematic comparison. This work structures the study of latent action supervision from two perspectives: (i) regularizing the trajectory via image-based latent actions, and (ii) unifying the target space with action-based latent actions. Under a unified VLA baseline, we instantiate and compare four representative integration strategies. Our results reveal a formulation-task correspondence: image-based latent actions benefit long-horizon reasoning and scene-level generalization, whereas action-based latent actions excel at complex motor coordination. Furthermore, we find that directly supervising the VLM with discrete latent action tokens yields the most effective performance. Finally, our experiments offer initial insights into the benefits of latent action supervision in mixed-data, suggesting a promising direction for VLA training. Code is available at https://github.com/RUCKBReasoning/From_Pixels_to_Tokens.

</details>


### [205] [3D Printing of Passively Actuated Self-Folding Robots with Integrated Functional Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04757)
*Gaolin Ge,Qifeng Yang,Haoran Lu,Tingyu Cheng,Martin Nisser,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弹性驱动自折叠方法，利用3D打印的导电PLA网和弹性带，将平板材料折叠成3D机器人结构，集成了传感、驱动和模块化能力，并建立了折叠角度预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现低成本、无外部刺激的自折叠机器人制造方法，避免传统气动、热或电磁驱动带来的复杂性和局限性，同时集成传感与驱动功能。

Method: 在3D打印的导电PLA平板上通过打印钩子路由弹性带，利用弹性带储存的能量驱动折叠；同一基板作为电容触摸电极，集成霍尔传感器和ERM电机实现对接检测和振动驱动；推导闭环折叠模型平衡铰链刚度与弹性带力矩以预测平衡折叠角。

Result: 实验验证了折叠模型的准确性，得出了铰链厚度、弹性带尺寸和钩间距与目标角度之间的设计映射；实现了多面体模块、立方体、可展开夹持器和腱驱动手指三种应用原型。

Conclusion: 该方法低成本、无需外部刺激，可直接从平板制造集成传感与驱动的机器人，尤其适用于可扩展模块化机器人集群的快速制备。

Abstract: We introduce an elastic-driven self-folding approach that fabricates robots directly from flat 3D-printed conductive PLA nets. Elastic bands routed through printed hooks store energy that folds the sheet into programmed 3D geometries, while the flat state allows accurate placement of electronics and magnets before deployment. The same substrate doubles as electrodes for capacitive touch and supports a reusable platform I/O palette with Hall sensors and eccentric rotating mass (ERM) motors for docking detection and vibration actuation. We also derive a closed-form folding model that balances hinge stiffness with elastic band moment to predict equilibrium fold angles; experiments validate the model and yield a design map linking hinge thickness, band size, and hook spacing to target angles. Using this workflow we realize multiple polyhedral modules and demonstrate three applications: a cube that highlights the potential of self-folding for scalable modular robot collectives, a deployable gripper, and a tendon-driven finger. The method is low cost, stimulus-free, and integrates actuation and sensing.

</details>


### [206] [Dr-PoGO: Direct Radar Pose-Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04806)
*Cedric Le Gentil,Weican Li,Leonardo Brizi,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dr-PoGO是一种利用2D旋转雷达进行SLAM的方法，通过直接配准技术和雷达专用地点识别算法，在恶劣天气条件下实现了优于现有方法的定位与建图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达能够穿透灰尘、雪、雨等恶劣天气，相较于相机和激光雷达具有更强的鲁棒性，但现有雷达SLAM方法多依赖点云或特征提取进行运动估计，存在局限性。本文旨在利用直接配准技术提升雷达SLAM在复杂环境下的性能。

Method: 1) 采用直接配准技术进行里程计估计(DRO)和回环检测；2) 使用雷达专用地点识别算法RaPlace提供回环候选；3) 提出粗到细的配准方法，利用视觉特征和描述子获得初始估计，再通过直接变换精化；4) 通过位姿图优化全局轨迹。

Result: 在超过300公里的真实汽车环境数据上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Dr-PoGO证明了直接配准技术在雷达SLAM中的有效性，在多种真实驾驶环境下优于现有方法，且代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-PoGO, a method for Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM) using a 2D spinning radar. Unlike cameras or lidars that require line-of-sight, millimetre-wave radars can `see' through dust, falling snow, rain, etc. Accordingly, it is a great modality for robust perception regardless of the weather conditions. While most existing radar-based SLAM methods rely on the extraction of point clouds or features to perform ego-motion estimation, Dr-PoGO leverages direct registration techniques for odometry (DRO) and loop-closure registration. An off-the-shelf radar-focused place recognition algorithm, RaPlace, provides loop-closure candidates. As RaPlace does not provide relative transformations, Dr-PoGO introduces a coarse-to-fine registration that uses visual features and descriptors to obtain an initial guess for the direct transformation refinement. The global trajectory is optimized in a pose-graph optimization. Dr-PoGO demonstrates state-of-the-art performance over 300km of data in various real-world automotive environments. Our implementation is publicly available: https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_pogo.

</details>


### [207] [Optimal Uncertainty-Aware Calibration for the AX=YB Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04809)
*Yanjia Chen,Xiangfei Li,Huan Zhao,Yiyuan Hong,Guanxiao Xia,Jiexin Zhang,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李代数的迭代优化框架用于手眼标定，该方法严格保持校准参数的结构约束，实现参数同步更新，并通过不确定性度量动态优化迭代过程，在高不确定性条件下精度提升至少67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手眼标定方法在过载和大工作空间工业机器人场景下，数据存在不确定性且难以精确建模，导致标定精度显著下降，因此需要一种无需显式不确定性建模但能有效应对不确定性的优化方法。

Method: 1) 基于李代数的迭代优化算法，实现近似全局最优解；2) 严格保持校准参数的结构约束并实现参数同步更新；3) 引入不确定性度量评估数据源间的相对不确定性，动态优化迭代过程；4) 设计有效的初始解生成方法提升收敛效率与稳定性。

Result: 数值仿真和真实实验验证了方法的有效性；在合成数据集中，高不确定性条件下，所提方法相比现有方法估计精度提升至少67%。

Conclusion: 所提出的优化框架能有效应对手眼标定中的数据不确定性问题，显著提升标定精度，尤其适用于高不确定性场景。

Abstract: This article proposes a general optimization framework for solving hand-eye calibration problem. Unlike traditional methods, an iterative algorithm based on Lie algebra that achieves approximately global optimal solutions is developed. During the optimization process, the method strictly preserves the structural constraints of the calibration parameters and enables synchronized updates between calibration parameters. Recognizing that data used in real-word hand-eye calibration often contain uncertainty, especially in over-loading and large workspace industrial robot scenarios, which can significantly degrade accuracy, and accurately modeling such uncertainty is inherently difficult, this article avoids explicit uncertainty modeling. Instead, an uncertainty metric to evaluate the relative uncertainty between data sources is introduced and used to dynamically refine the iterative process. To further enhance convergence efficiency, an effective initial solution generation method that improves overall stability and accuracy is designed. Numerical simulations and real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, and in synthetic datasets, the proposed approach improves the estimation accuracy by at least 67\% under high-uncertainty conditions compared with the existing methods.

</details>


### [208] [Modular Reinforcement Learning For Cooperative Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04939)
*Erel Shtossel,Gal A. Kaminka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于群体机器人强化学习的模块化解耦状态表示方法，将空间交互状态的每个特征由独立的学习过程处理并聚合结果，解决了传统方法中机器人需要表示组合数量交互状态所带来的内存挑战，并通过模拟觅食实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人中每个机器人只能与少量同伴交互且不知晓集体效用，现有分布式多智能体强化学习方法虽能使机器人独立学习有效交互，但需要表示潜在的组合数量交互状态，挑战了机器人的有限内存能力。

Method: 提出模块化（解耦）表示方法，将状态空间的每个特征交由独立的学习过程处理，然后将结果进行聚合，从而避免表示组合数量的交互状态。

Result: 在模拟群体机器人觅食任务的多项实验中证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 模块化解耦表示能够有效克服机器人内存限制，使群体机器人通过独立强化学习实现与共同目标一致的协作行为。

Abstract: A cooperative robot swarm is a collective of computationally-limited robots that share a common goal. Each robot can only interact with a small subset of its peers, without knowing how this affects the collective utility. Recent advances in distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning have demonstrated that it is possible for robots to learn how to interact effectively with others, in a manner that is aligned with the common goal, despite each robot learning independently of others. However, this requires each robot to represent a potentially combinatorial number of interaction states, challenging the memory capabilities of the robots. This paper proposes an alternative approach for representing spatial interaction states for multi-robot reinforcement learning in swarms. A modular (decomposed) representation is used, where each feature of the state is handled by a separate learning procedure, and the results aggregated. We demonstrate the efficacy of the approach in numerous experiments with simulated robot swarms carrying out foraging.

</details>


### [209] [Reduced-order Neural Modeling with Differentiable Simulation for High-Detail Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05053)
*Yuhu Guo,Zhikai Shen,Jiasheng Qu,Chenghao Qian,Yuming Huang,Bin Chen,Guoxing Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降阶神经仿真框架，将粗粒度MPM动力学与隐式神经解码器耦合，从紧凑潜在状态重建亚粒子触觉细节，相比TacIPC实现65%以上仿真加速和40%更低内存使用，同时保持更高几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率弹性体变形仿真计算成本过高：FEM精度高但需要昂贵的网格重划分，MPM则面临粒子-内存权衡问题，现有方法难以在效率和精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出降阶神经仿真框架，耦合粗粒度MPM动力学与隐式神经解码器，从配对的高低分辨率仿真中学习连续变形流形，实现物理一致的可微推理。

Result: 相比TacIPC，仿真速度提升65%以上，内存使用降低40%；触觉渲染和3D表面重建精度提高25%，生成更真实的深度图像和表面网格且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 所提出的降阶神经模型能够实现高细节、物理一致的触觉仿真，为机器人交互和优化任务带来显著的效率提升。

Abstract: Tactile perception is key to dexterous manipulation, yet simulating high-resolution elastomer deformation remains computationally prohibitive. Finite element methods (FEM) deliver high fidelity but demand costly remeshing, while Material Point Methods (MPM) suffer from heavy particle-memory tradeoffs. We propose a {reduced-order neural simulation framework} that couples coarse-grained MPM dynamics with an implicit neural decoder to reconstruct sub-particle tactile details from compact latent states. The framework learns a continuous deformation manifold from paired high- and low-resolution simulations, enabling physically consistent, differentiable inference. Compared to the TacIPC, our method achieves over 65\% faster simulation and {40\% lower memory usage}, while maintaining better geometric fidelity. In tactile rendering and 3D surface reconstruction, our methods further improve accuracy by 25\% and produce realistic depth images and surface mesh within a faster inference speed. These results demonstrate that the proposed reduced-order neural model enables high-detail, physically grounded tactile simulation with substantial efficiency gains for robotic interaction and optimization.

</details>


### [210] [Driver-WM: A Driver-Centric Traffic-Conditioned Latent World Model for In-Cabin Dynamics Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05092)
*Haozhuang Chi,Daosheng Qiu,Hao Su,Haochen Liu,Zirui Li,Haoruo Zhang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Driver-WM，一种以驾驶员为中心的潜在世界模型，在紧密的潜在空间中通过双流架构和门控因果注入机制，实现对外部交通环境因果条件下驾驶室内动力学的多步展开预测，统一了几何运动预测与行为/情绪语义识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型主要关注外部环境预测，而舱内智能仅停留在识别层面，缺乏对驾驶员动态的多步展开能力，无法满足L2/L3级自动驾驶中人在环共享控制过渡的预测需求。

Method: 提出Driver-WM：1) 基于冻结视觉-语言特征构建紧凑潜在空间；2) 双流架构分别编码外部交通和内部驾驶员状态；3) 门控因果注入机制实现外部到内部的定向耦合，通过学习的向量门控调制外部上下文扰动并严格维持时序因果性。

Result: 在多任务辅助驾驶基准上，Driver-WM在反应性高运动机动中实现了鲁棒的长时间几何预测，并改善了驾驶员与交通状态的语义对齐。

Conclusion: 显式的外部到内部条件建模允许受控的测试时干预，可系统分析机制响应，为安全的人机共享控制过渡提供支撑。

Abstract: Safe L2/L3 driving automation requires anticipating human-in-the-loop reactions during shared-control transitions. While most driving world models forecast the external environment, in-cabin intelligence remains strictly recognition-oriented and lacks multi-step rollout capabilities for driver dynamics. We introduce Driver-WM, a driver-centric latent world model that rolls out in-cabin dynamics causally conditioned on out-cabin traffic context. This formulation unifies physical kinematics forecasting with auxiliary behavioral and emotional semantic recognition. Operating in a compact latent space constructed from frozen vision-language features, Driver-WM adopts a dual-stream architecture to separately encode external traffic and internal driver states. These streams are directionally coupled via a gated causal injection mechanism, which uses a learned vector gate to modulate external contextual perturbations while strictly enforcing temporal causality. Evaluations on a multi-task assistive driving benchmark demonstrate that Driver-WM yields robust long-horizon geometric forecasting for reactive high-motion maneuvers and improves semantic alignment for both driver and traffic states. Finally, the explicit external-to-internal conditioning allows for controlled test-time interventions to systematically analyze mechanism responses.

</details>


### [211] [LineRides: Line-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Bicycle Robot Stunts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05110)
*Seungeun Rho,Shamel Fahmi,Jeonghwan Kim,Arianna Ilvonen,Sehoon Ha,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LineRides是一种基于空间引导线的强化学习框架，无需演示或显式时间控制，即可让自行车机器人在无参考运动数据的情况下习得多达五种可命令执行的敏捷特技动作（MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip、DriftTurn），并实现与正常行驶的平滑切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中为敏捷机器人动作设计奖励函数十分困难，而基于演示的方法需要参考运动数据，但对于新型平台或极限特技场景往往无法获得此类数据。

Method: 提出LineRides框架，核心包括：(1) 用户提供空间引导线（guideline）和稀疏关键朝向（key-orientations）；(2) 引入跟踪余量（tracking margin）处理物理不可行的引导线；(3) 通过沿引导线行驶的距离度量进度以消除时间歧义；(4) 利用位置和序列关键朝向消除运动细节歧义。训练基于强化学习。

Result: 在Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV)平台上，训练的策略支持正常行驶与特技执行之间的无缝切换，成功实现了五种不同特技：MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip和DriftTurn。

Conclusion: LineRides有效解决了无演示场景下敏捷特技任务的奖励设计难题，验证了基于引导线的学习框架在物理可行性与运动多样性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Designing reward functions for agile robotic maneuvers in reinforcement learning remains difficult, and demonstration-based approaches often require reference motions that are unavailable for novel platforms or extreme stunts. We present LineRides, a line-guided learning framework that enables a custom bicycle robot to acquire diverse, commandable stunt behaviors from a user-provided spatial guideline and sparse key-orientations, without demonstrations or explicit timing. LineRides handles physically infeasible guidelines using a tracking margin that permits controlled deviation, resolves temporal ambiguity by measuring progress via traveled distance along the guideline, and disambiguates motion details through position- and sequence-based key-orientations. We evaluate LineRides on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV) and show that the policy trained with our methods supports seamless transitions between normal driving and stunt execution, enabling five distinct stunts on command: MiniHop, LargeHop, ThreePointTurn, Backflip, and DriftTurn.

</details>


### [212] [ConsisVLA-4D: Advancing Spatiotemporal Consistency in Efficient 3D-Perception and 4D-Reasoning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05126)
*Wei Li,Jizhihui Liu,Li Yixing,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConsisVLA-4D 是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过 CV-Aligner、CO-Fuser 和 CS-Thinker 三个模块分别解决跨视图物体语义一致性、跨对象空间几何一致性和跨场景时空一致性，在 LIBERO 基准和真实平台上相比 OpenVLA 分别提升 21.6%/41.5% 性能，推理速度提升 2.3/2.4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型存在两个主要问题：1) 空间表示依赖额外传感器，计算开销大；2) 视觉推理仅限于未来帧预测，缺乏与指令对齐的场景一致性，导致时空一致性差。本文旨在解决这些问题，提升 3D 感知和 4D 推理的时空一致性。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：1) CV-Aligner，通过过滤指令相关区域并对齐多视角物体身份，确保跨视图语义一致性；2) CO-Fuser，利用紧凑潜在表示消除跨视图物体间空间关系歧义，保证跨对象空间几何一致性；3) CS-Thinker，从 CV-Aligner 的物体语义令牌和 CO-Fuser 的几何令牌中学习局部动态和全局深度的隐式知识，实现跨场景时空一致性。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上性能提升 21.6%，推理加速 2.3 倍；在真实平台上性能提升 41.5%，推理加速 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: ConsisVLA-4D 通过高效的时空一致性设计，显著提升了 VLA 模型的感知与推理能力，同时大幅加速推理，验证了其在 3D 感知和 4D 推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models primarily focus on mapping 2D observations to actions, but exhibit notable limitations in spatiotemporal perception and reasoning: 1) spatial representations often rely on additional sensors, introducing substantial computational overhead; 2) visual reasoning is typically limited to future-frame prediction, lacking alignment with the instruction-grounded scene and thus compromising spatiotemporal consistency. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisVLA-4D, a unified and efficient framework that enhances spatiotemporal consistency in 3D perception and 4D reasoning. Specifically, we design: 1) CV-Aligner, which ensures cross-view object semantic consistency by filtering instruction-relevant regions and aligning object identities across multiple viewpoints; 2) CO-Fuser, which guarantees cross-object spatial geometric consistency by eliminating spatial relation ambiguities between objects across views using compact latent representations. Building upon these, we introduce 3) CS-Thinker to achieve cross-scene spatiotemporal consistency as actions unfold. It learns implicit knowledge of local dynamics from object-semantic tokens of CV-Aligner and global depth from geometric tokens of CO-Fuser, thereby enhancing efficient visual reasoning under scene variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, benefiting from its efficient spatiotemporal consistency design, ConsisVLA-4D achieves 21.6% and 41.5% performance improvements, along with 2.3-fold and 2.4-fold inference speedups compared to OpenVLA on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world platforms, respectively.ConsisVLA-4D is open-sourced and publicly available at

</details>


### [213] [When Life Gives You BC, Make Q-functions: Extracting Q-values from Behavior Cloning for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05172)
*Lakshita Dodeja,Ondrej Biza,Shivam Vats,Stephen Hart,Stefanie Tellex,Robin Walters,Karl Schmeckpeper,Thomas Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q2RL算法，通过Q值估计和Q值门控机制，实现从行为克隆到强化学习的高效离线到在线转换，在机器人操作任务中达到100%成功率和最高3.75倍于原始BC策略的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆缺乏在线自我改进机制，现有离线到在线学习方法存在离线数据与在线学习之间的分布不匹配问题，导致策略遗忘已学好的动作。

Method: Q2RL包含两部分：(1) Q-Estimation：通过与环境的少量交互从BC策略中提取Q函数；(2) Q-Gating：基于Q值在BC和RL策略动作之间切换，为RL策略训练收集样本。

Result: 在D4RL和robomimic基准的多项操作任务中，Q2RL在成功率和收敛时间上优于现有离线到在线学习基线；在机器人实物上，1-2小时在线交互内达到100%成功率，相比原始BC策略提升最高3.75倍。

Conclusion: Q2RL是一种高效的离线到在线学习算法，能够快速将BC策略提升为高性能RL策略，适用于接触丰富和高精度操作任务。

Abstract: Behavior Cloning (BC) has emerged as a highly effective paradigm for robot learning. However, BC lacks a self-guided mechanism for online improvement after demonstrations have been collected. Existing offline-to-online learning methods often cause policies to replace previously learned good actions due to a distribution mismatch between offline data and online learning. In this work, we propose Q2RL, Q-Estimation and Q-Gating from BC for Reinforcement Learning, an algorithm for efficient offline-to-online learning. Our method consists of two parts: (1) Q-Estimation extracts a Q-function from a BC policy using a few interaction steps with the environment, followed by online RL with (2) Q-Gating, which switches between BC and RL policy actions based on their respective Q-values to collect samples for RL policy training. Across manipulation tasks from D4RL and robomimic benchmarks, Q2RL outperforms SOTA offline-to-online learning baselines on success rate and time to convergence. Q2RL is efficient enough to be applied in an on-robot RL setting, learning robust policies for contact-rich and high precision manipulation tasks such as pipe assembly and kitting, in 1-2 hours of online interaction, achieving success rates of up to 100% and up to 3.75x improvement against the original BC policy. Code and video are available at https://pages.rai-inst.com/q2rl_website/

</details>


### [214] [A Closed-Form Dual-Barrier CBF Safety Filter for Holonomic Robots on Incrementally Built Occupancy Grid Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05182)
*Himanshu Paudel,Basanta Joshi,Dhirendra Raj Madai,Alina Bartaula,Biman Rimal,Sanjay Neupane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双重障碍函数的安全滤波器，用于全向移动机器人在增量构建占据栅格地图中的实时速度控制。该滤波器同时约束已测绘障碍物和未探索区域，通过占据栅格符号距离场推导出闭式解，并在资源受限平台（如树莓派）上实现低开销运行。自适应增益调度在信息丰富区域放松前沿约束、在良好测绘区域收紧约束，以提高探索效率。硬件飞行实验在PX4四旋翼上实现了零碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未知环境探索时，未测绘区域存在不可约的不确定性，进入这些区域会带来碰撞风险，尤其是前向传感器场景下。现有方法缺乏同时处理已测绘障碍和未探索区域约束的轻量级安全滤波方案。

Method: 采用双重障碍函数安全滤波器，两个约束（已测绘障碍物避碰、未探索区域限制）均从占据栅格的符号距离场解析推导，得到闭式安全滤波器，每周期仅需求解一个小型线性系统。设计了自适应增益调度机制，根据信息丰富程度动态调整前沿约束的松紧度。

Result: 在资源受限的树莓派平台上计算开销低，与SLAM和规划模块共存时资源占用少；在PX4控制的四旋翼室内飞行实验中实现零碰撞。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为最小干预的修正器在速度空间运行，兼容任意名义控制器（包括学习方法），在保证安全的同时提升探索效率，适合实时安全关键应用。

Abstract: We present a dual-barrier control barrier function (CBF) safety filter for real-time, safety-critical velocity control of holonomic robots operating in incrementally built occupancy grid maps. As a robot explores an unknown environment, unmapped regions introduce irreducible uncertainty, since obstacle geometry beyond the explored frontier is unknown, making entry into such regions a source of collision risk, especially with front-facing sensors. To address this, we enforce two constraints: avoidance of mapped obstacles and restriction from unexplored regions. Both constraints are derived analytically from the occupancy grid's signed distance field, yielding a closed-form safety filter that requires only a small linear system solve per cycle. On resource-constrained platforms such as the Raspberry Pi, where SLAM and planning already consume significant compute, the low overhead of the proposed filter preserves resources. An adaptive gain schedule relaxes the frontier constraint in information-rich regions and tightens it in well-mapped areas, improving exploration efficiency while maintaining safety. The filter operates in velocity space as a minimally invasive correction and composes with arbitrary nominal controllers, including learning-based methods. Hardware flight experiments on a PX4-controlled quadrotor demonstrate zero collisions across multiple indoor runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [LCM: Lossless Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050)
*Clint Ehrlich,Theodore Blackman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Lossless Context Management (LCM)，一种用于LLM记忆的确定性架构，通过递归上下文压缩（层次化摘要DAG）和递归任务划分（LLM-Map等引擎管理原语）提升长上下文任务性能，在OOLONG评测中超越Claude Code。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在处理超长上下文时性能下降，即使具备本地文件系统访问能力的顶级编码智能体也存在局限。作者希望通过确定性递归机制替代传统方法，在保证终止性、连续性和可检索性的前提下提升长上下文任务表现。

Method: LCM将符号递归分解为两种确定性引擎管理机制：(1) 递归上下文压缩——构建层次化摘要DAG，自动压缩旧消息同时保留无损指针；(2) 递归任务划分——使用引擎管理的并行原语（如LLM-Map）替代模型编写的循环。

Result: 基于Opus 4.6的LCM增强编码智能体Volt在OOLONG长上下文评测中全面超越Claude Code，涵盖32K至1M tokens的每个上下文长度区间。

Conclusion: LCM不仅是递归语言模型范式的验证与扩展，也证明递归上下文操作能超越传统LLM和拥有本地文件系统访问的前沿编码智能体。LCM牺牲了最大灵活性，换取了终止保证、短任务零开销连续性和所有历史状态的无损可检索性。

Abstract: We introduce Lossless Context Management (LCM), a deterministic architecture for LLM memory that outperforms Claude Code on long-context tasks. When benchmarked using Opus 4.6, our LCM-augmented coding agent, Volt, achieves higher scores than Claude Code on the OOLONG long-context eval, including at every context length between 32K and 1M tokens.
  LCM may be considered both a vindication and extension of the recursive paradigm pioneered by Recursive Language Models (RLMs). Our results demonstrate that recursive context manipulation can outperform not just conventional LLMs, but frontier coding agents with native file-system access.
  LCM departs from RLM by decomposing symbolic recursion into two deterministic, engine-managed mechanisms: recursive context compression, in which a hierarchical summary DAG automatically compacts older messages while retaining lossless pointers to every original; and recursive task partitioning, in which engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map replace model-written loops. This trade-off, analogous to the move from GOTO to structured control flow in program-ming language design, sacrifices maximal flexibility for termination guarantees, zero-cost continuity on short tasks, and lossless retrievability of all prior state.

</details>


### [216] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），通过在强调TD方法中引入受控正则化项解决非策略TD学习中的稳定性、投影几何与方差控制之间的权衡问题，理论证明收敛性并在线性非策略预测任务中验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非策略时序差分学习面临稳定性、投影几何与方差控制之间的结构性权衡。强调TD通过后续强调改善了非策略投影几何，但后续迹存在高方差问题。本文旨在通过Bellman误差中心化重新审视这一权衡，并解决朴素中心化强调扩展中出现的正定性破坏问题。

Method: 提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），保留后续迹的同时仅对辅助中心化递归施加正则化，将耦合关键矩阵的右下角块从1提升到1+c。推导了RETD核心矩阵，并在保守的充分正则化条件下证明收敛性。

Result: 实验表明RETD避免了朴素中心化强调学习的不稳定性，保留了良好的强调几何特性，并在多个诊断任务中对正则化参数c表现出稳健的中间区间。

Conclusion: RETD通过受控正则化有效解决了非策略TD学习中的关键权衡问题，在保持强调投影几何优势的同时增强了稳定性，为非线性扩展提供了基础。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [217] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANDRE是一种基于注意力的神经符号可微规则提取器，通过可微的注意力驱动逻辑算子替代传统规则模板和模糊算子，在连续规则空间中学习一阶逻辑程序，在噪声和概率性数据上实现了优于现有可微ILP方法的规则提取质量和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号和神经符号ILP方法难以扩展到噪声和概率性场景：经典ILP依赖离散组合搜索且对不确定性脆弱，可微ILP方法则受限于预定义规则模板或不精确的模糊算子（存在梯度消失或逻辑结构近似差的问题）。

Method: 提出ANDRE框架，采用可微的注意力驱动合取和析取算子来近似逻辑min-max语义，在连续规则空间中进行优化；支持规则内谓词的软选择、否定或排除，保持符号结构的同时实现灵活的规则归纳。

Result: 在经典ILP基准、大规模知识库和含概率谓词及噪声监督的合成数据集上，ANDRE达到了竞争性或更优的预测性能，能可靠地恢复正确符号规则；在中等标签噪声下保持鲁棒性，在规则提取质量和稳定性上显著优于现有可微ILP方法。

Conclusion: ANDRE通过注意力机制实现了完全可微的逻辑推理，有效弥合了符号ILP的离散性与概率数据不确定性之间的鸿沟，为噪声场景下的可解释规则学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [218] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pro²Assist，一种基于AR眼镜的多模态步进感知主动助手，能持续追踪细粒度任务进度并推理用户需求，在程序性动作理解准确率上超基线21%，主动时机准确率达基线的2.29倍，用户研究显示90%参与者认为有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM驱动的个人助手主要提供被动响应式指导或对孤立短期事件的有限主动协助，缺乏对长期程序性任务的连续感知与主动辅助能力。

Method: 利用AR眼镜多模态数据进行运动感知，从多尺度时间动态和任务特定专家知识中提取面向步骤的程序上下文，基于感官输入与程序上下文进行连续推理以推断用户需求，并在AR眼镜上显示及时辅助信息。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实AR眼镜数据集上，程序性动作理解准确率超基线21%以上，主动时机准确率达基线2.29倍；20人用户研究中90%认为有用。

Conclusion: Pro²Assist在长期程序性任务的主动辅助方面显著优于现有方法，用户研究验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [219] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率不一致信号（PIS）的神经符号问答框架，通过将非结构化文本显式提升为事件图和区间约束，解耦语义提取与符号推理。实验表明，当提供正确结构表示时，系统在时序算术基准上达到完美1.0准确率，从根本上将时序问答从算法推理问题重构为结构对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"大语言模型在复杂时序推理任务中表现脆弱是由于自回归逻辑推理存在固有缺陷"这一主流观点，证明真正的瓶颈在于非结构化文本到事件表示的转换过程，而非推理能力本身。

Method: 提出神经符号问答框架：(1) 将非结构化文本显式提升为事件图与区间约束，严格解耦语义提取与符号推理引擎；(2) 引入概率不一致信号（PIS），通过融合符号信度区间与基于证据深度学习从LLM隐藏状态提取的认知神经不确定性，鲁棒地检测结构断裂点。

Result: 在时序算术基准上，当提供正确结构表示时，系统实现完美1.0准确率（4000/4000），严格零假阳性与假阴性；在更广泛的噪声注入QA设置下，框架保持75.1%的竞争性准确率，同时支持确定性的、步骤级别的故障定位。

Conclusion: 时序推理的根本瓶颈在于表示而非推理。通过将表示瓶颈与推理基质分离，本文将时序问答从算法推理挑战重构为结构对齐问题，为可验证的可靠神经符号AI指明了方向。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction. In this paper, we challenge this prevailing narrative, demonstrating that temporal reasoning is not the fundamental bottleneck; rather, the locus of failure lies in unstructured text-to-event representation. We introduce a novel neuro-symbolic question-answering framework governed by a Probabilistic Inconsistency Signal (PIS) that explicitly isolates perceptual errors from reasoning failures. By lifting unstructured text into explicit event graphs and interval constraints, our architecture strictly decouples semantic extraction from a symbolic reasoning engine. To robustly detect structural breaks, the PIS elegantly unifies symbolic credal intervals with epistemic neural uncertainty extracted via Evidential Deep Learning on LLM hidden states. Empirical evaluations reveal a striking paradigm shift: when provided with correct structural representations, our system's explicit proof traces achieve perfect 1.0 accuracy (4000/4000) and strictly zero false positives/negatives on temporal arithmetic benchmarks. On broader, noise-injected QA settings, the framework maintains a competitive 75.1\% accuracy while enabling deterministic, step-level failure localization. Ultimately, by isolating the representation bottleneck from the reasoning substrate, this work reframes temporal QA from an algorithmic reasoning challenge to a structural alignment problem, charting a verifiable path forward for reliable neuro-symbolic AI.

</details>


### [220] [Parallel Prefix Verification for Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04263)
*Yuncheng Yao,Yuxuan Xia,Shengjie Wang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSE是一种并行前缀验证的推测解码框架，通过语义级并行验证替代逐token验证，将LLM推理吞吐量提升1.25-4.3倍，与EAGLE-3组合可达1.6-4.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法受限于token级等价验证，导致接受长度短、加速有限；而语义级/段级验证虽能增大接受粒度，但依赖顺序验证带来显著开销。因此需要一种并行化的语义级验证方法。

Method: 提出PARSE框架，核心是并行前缀验证：给定草稿模型的完整草稿，目标模型通过自定义注意力掩码在单次前向传播中同时评估多个前缀的正确性，直接识别最大有效前缀，无需顺序段验证。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上，PARSE相比目标模型实现1.25倍至4.3倍的吞吐量提升；与EAGLE-3组合时提升至1.6倍至4.5倍，且准确率几乎无损。

Conclusion: 并行前缀验证是一种有效且通用的LLM推理加速方法，与token级推测解码正交并可组合使用。

Abstract: We introduce PARSE (PArallel pRefix Speculative Engine), a speculative generation framework that accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by parallelizing prefix verification on a semantic level. Existing speculative decoding methods are fundamentally limited by token-level equivalence: the target model must verify each token, leading to short acceptance lengths and modest speedups. Moving to semantic or segment-level verification can substantially increase acceptance granularity, but prior approaches rely on sequential verification, introducing significant overhead and limiting practical gains. PARSE introduces parallel prefix verification, enabling semantic-level verification without sequential checks. Given a full draft from a draft model, the target model evaluates correctness across multiple prefixes in a single forward pass using a custom attention mask, directly identifying the maximal valid prefix. This eliminates sequential segment verification, and makes verification compute-efficient. PARSE is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding and can be composed with it for additional gains. Across models and benchmarks, PARSE delivers $1.25\times$ to $4.3\times$ throughput gain over the target model, and $1.6\times$ to $4.5\times$ when composed with EAGLE-3, all with negligible accuracy degradation. This demonstrates parallel prefix verification as an effective, general approach to accelerating LLM inference.

</details>


### [221] [The Scaling Properties of Implicit Deductive Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04330)
*Enrico Vompa,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度受限Transformer在Horn子句隐式演绎推理中的缩放性质。通过系统性地将可证明性与虚假特征解耦并强制执行算法对齐，发现具有双向前缀掩码的足够深度模型中，隐式推理在各种图拓扑和问题宽度上接近显式思维链（CoT）性能，但CoT在深度外推场景中仍然不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度受限Transformer在隐式演绎推理（Horn子句）中的缩放性质，并厘清隐式推理与显式思维链（CoT）在不同拓扑和宽度条件下的性能边界。

Method: 采用系统性解耦策略，将可证明性与虚假特征分离，并强制执行算法对齐；使用双向前缀掩码的深度模型进行实验，对比隐式推理与显式CoT在不同图拓扑和问题宽度下的表现。

Result: 在足够深度的模型中，隐式推理的性能接近显式CoT；但在深度外推任务中，CoT仍具不可替代的优势。

Conclusion: 隐式推理在足够深度且配置合理的模型中可以逼近显式CoT性能，但CoT在深度外推场景下仍是必要组件。

Abstract: We investigate the scaling properties of implicit deductive reasoning over Horn clauses in depth-bounded Transformers. By systematically decorrelating provability from spurious features and enforcing algorithmic alignment, we find that in sufficiently deep models with a bidirectional prefix mask, implicit reasoning approaches explicit CoT performance across graph topologies and problem widths, though CoT remains necessary for depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [222] [When Context Hurts: The Crossover Effect of Knowledge Transfer on Multi-Agent Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04361)
*Saranyan Vigraham*

Main category: cs.AI
